ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\app\(tabs)\assistant.tsx

import React, { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, Pressable, ScrollView, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import type { ChatMessage } from '@finzen/core';
import { AppText, Card, Chip } from '../../src/components/ui';
import { useFocusData } from '../../src/hooks/useFocusData';
import { askAssistant, isAiConfigured } from '../../src/services/ai';
import { useTheme } from '../../src/theme/ThemeProvider';

const SUGGESTIONS = [
  'Â¿CuÃ¡nto gastÃ© en comida este mes?',
  'Â¿Voy bien con mi presupuesto?',
  'Â¿QuÃ© deuda deberÃ­a pagar primero?',
  'Â¿DÃ³nde puedo recortar gastos?',
];

export default function Assistant() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [configured] = useFocusData(isAiConfigured, true);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const scroll = useRef<ScrollView>(null);

  const send = async (text: string) => {
    const q = text.trim();
    if (!q || busy) return;
    setInput(''); setError(null); setBusy(true);
    const history = messages;
    setMessages([...history, { role: 'user', content: q }]);
    try {
      const answer = await askAssistant(q, history);
      setMessages((m) => [...m, { role: 'assistant', content: answer }]);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
      setTimeout(() => scroll.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: true }), 100);
    }
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <KeyboardAvoidingView style={{ flex: 1 }} behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingHorizontal: 20, paddingTop: 12 }}>
          <AppText variant="title">Asistente</AppText>
          <Pressable onPress={() => router.push('/assistant-settings')} hitSlop={12} accessibilityLabel="Configurar IA">
            <Ionicons name="settings-outline" size={24} color={colors.primary} />
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <ScrollView ref={scroll} contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 10 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
          {!configured && (
            <Card style={{ borderColor: colors.warning }}>
              <AppText color={colors.warning}>El asistente necesita una IA configurada.</AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>
                Toca el engranaje âš™ y pega una clave gratuita de Gemini (Google AI Studio), o la direcciÃ³n de tu Ollama. El resto de la app funciona sin esto.
              </AppText>
            </Card>
          )}
          {messages.length === 0 && (
            <View style={{ gap: 8 }}>
              <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>PregÃºntame sobre tus finanzas. Solo veo totales, sin nombres de cuentas, bancos ni comercios.</AppText>
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
                {SUGGESTIONS.map((s) => <Chip key={s} label={s} selected={false} onPress={() => send(s)} />)}
              </View>
            </View>
          )}
          {messages.map((m, i) => (
            <View key={i} style={{ alignSelf: m.role === 'user' ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start', maxWidth: '88%' }}>
              <Card style={{ backgroundColor: m.role === 'user' ? colors.primary : colors.surface, borderColor: m.role === 'user' ? colors.primary : colors.border }}>
                <AppText color={m.role === 'user' ? colors.onPrimary : colors.text}>{m.content}</AppText>
              </Card>
            </View>
          ))}
          {busy && <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>Pensandoâ€¦</AppText>}
          {error && <AppText color={colors.danger}>{error}</AppText>}
        </ScrollView>

        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, padding: 12, borderTopWidth: 1, borderTopColor: colors.border, backgroundColor: colors.surface }}>
          <TextInput
            value={input} onChangeText={setInput} placeholder="Escribe tu preguntaâ€¦" placeholderTextColor={colors.textSecondary}
            onSubmitEditing={() => send(input)} returnKeyType="send" multiline
            style={{ flex: 1, color: colors.text, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.border, borderRadius: 20, paddingHorizontal: 14, paddingVertical: 8, maxHeight: 100, fontSize: 16 }}
          />
          <Pressable onPress={() => send(input)} disabled={busy || !input.trim()}
            style={{ width: 46, height: 46, borderRadius: 23, backgroundColor: colors.primary, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', opacity: busy || !input.trim() ? 0.4 : 1 }}>
            <Ionicons name="send" size={20} color={colors.onPrimary} />
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\app\(tabs)\debts.tsx
==========================================
import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { rankDebts } from '@finzen/core';
import { ProgressBar } from '../../src/components/charts';
import { AppText, Button, Card } from '../../src/components/ui';
import { useFocusData } from '../../src/hooks/useFocusData';
import {
  DEBT_KIND_LABEL, cardSummary, listDebts, listPlansOf, upcomingPayments, type Debt,
} from '../../src/services/debts';
import { listPending } from '../../src/services/transactions';
import { useTheme } from '../../src/theme/ThemeProvider';
import { formatCOP } from '../../src/utils/format';

interface Overview {
  debts: Debt[];
  usage: Record<string, { used: number; available: number; utilization: number; nextDue: string | null }>;
  planCount: Record<string, number>;
  upcoming: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof upcomingPayments>>;
  pending: number;
}

async function loadOverview(): Promise<Overview> {
  const debts = await listDebts();
  const usage: Overview['usage'] = {};
  const planCount: Overview['planCount'] = {};
  for (const d of debts) {
    if (d.kind === 'credit_card') {
      const s = await cardSummary(d);
      usage[d.id] = { used: s.used, available: s.available, utilization: s.utilization, nextDue: s.nextDue };
      planCount[d.id] = (await listPlansOf(d.id)).filter((p) => p.status === 'active').length;
    }
  }
  return { debts, usage, planCount, upcoming: await upcomingPayments(4), pending: (await listPending()).length };
}

export default function Debts() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [o] = useFocusData<Overview>(loadOverview, { debts: [], usage: {}, planCount: {}, upcoming: [], pending: 0 });

  const ranking = rankDebts(o.debts.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, balance: d.balance, monthlyRate: d.monthly_rate })), 'avalanche');
  const first = ranking[0] ? o.debts.find((d) => d.id === ranking[0]!.id) : null;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 14 }}>
        <AppText variant="title">Deudas y tarjetas</AppText>

        {o.pending > 0 && (
          <Pressable onPress={() => router.push('/pending')}>
            <Card style={{ borderColor: colors.warning }}>
              <AppText color={colors.warning}>âš  {o.pending} compra(s) a crÃ©dito por completar</AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Toca para indicar en cuÃ¡ntas cuotas fueron.</AppText>
            </Card>
          </Pressable>
        )}

        {o.upcoming.length > 0 && (
          <Card>
            <AppText variant="subtitle">PrÃ³ximos pagos</AppText>
            {o.upcoming.map((u) => (
              <View key={u.installmentId} style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                <AppText variant="caption">{u.dueDate} Â· {u.debtName} {u.of ? `(${u.number}/${u.of})` : ''}</AppText>
                <AppText variant="caption">{formatCOP(u.total)}</AppText>
              </View>
            ))}
          </Card>
        )}

        {first && o.debts.length > 1 && (
          <Card>
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>MÃ©todo avalancha: si tienes dinero extra, abona primero a</AppText>
            <AppText variant="subtitle" color={colors.primary}>{first.name}</AppText>
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>
              Es la de mayor interÃ©s ({(first.monthly_rate * 100).toFixed(2)} % mensual).
            </AppText>
          </Card>
        )}

        {o.debts.map((d) => {
          const u = o.usage[d.id];
          return (
            <Pressable key={d.id} onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: '/debt/[id]', params: { id: d.id } })}>
              <Card>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                  <View style={{ flexShrink: 1 }}>
                    <AppText variant="subtitle">{d.name}</AppText>
                    <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>{DEBT_KIND_LABEL[d.kind]}</AppText>
                  </View>
                  <AppText color={colors.danger}>{formatCOP(u ? u.used : d.balance)}</AppText>
                </View>
                {u && d.credit_limit ? (
                  <>
                    <ProgressBar ratio={u.utilization} level={u.utilization > 1 ? 'over' : u.utilization >= 0.8 ? 'warn' : 'ok'} />
                    <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>
                      Disponible {formatCOP(u.available)} de {formatCOP(d.credit_limit)}
                      {o.planCount[d.id] ? ` Â· ${o.planCount[d.id]} compra(s) a cuotas` : ''}
                    </AppText>
                    {u.nextDue && <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>PrÃ³ximo pago: {u.nextDue}</AppText>}
                  </>
                ) : (
                  d.monthly_payment ? <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Cuota {formatCOP(d.monthly_payment)}{d.due_day ? ` Â· dÃ­a ${d.due_day}` : ''}</AppText> : null
                )}
              </Card>
            </Pressable>
          );
        })}

        {o.debts.length === 0 && <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>AÃºn no tienes deudas registradas.</AppText>}
        <Button label="Agregar tarjeta o deuda" onPress={() => router.push('/debt/new')} />
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\app\(tabs)\index.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, ScrollView, View, useWindowDimensions } from 'react-native';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { budgetStatus, monthKey } from '@finzen/core';
import { DonutChart, LineChart, ProgressBar } from '../../src/components/charts';
import { AppText, Card, InfoRow } from '../../src/components/ui';
import { useFocusData } from '../../src/hooks/useFocusData';
import { listSnapshots, recordSnapshot, type NetWorth, type Snapshot } from '../../src/services/repo';
import {
  listBudgetLines, listPending, listTransactions, monthTotals, spendingByCategory,
  type BudgetLine, type CategorySpend, type MonthTotals, type TxRow,
} from '../../src/services/transactions';
import { listDrafts } from '../../src/services/notifications';
import { getTotalRecurring } from '../../src/services/recurring'; // NUEVO IMPORT
import { upcomingPayments, type UpcomingPayment } from '../../src/services/debts';
import { useTheme } from '../../src/theme/ThemeProvider';
import { formatCOP } from '../../src/utils/format';

interface Dash {
  nw: NetWorth; snaps: Snapshot[]; totals: MonthTotals; spend: CategorySpend[];
  budgets: BudgetLine[]; recent: TxRow[]; pending: number; drafts: number; upcoming: UpcomingPayment[];
  recurringTotal: number; // NUEVO
}
const EMPTY: Dash = {
  nw: { assets: 0, liabilities: 0, net: 0 }, snaps: [], totals: { income: 0, expense: 0, debtPayments: 0 },
  spend: [], budgets: [], recent: [], pending: 0, drafts: 0, upcoming: [], recurringTotal: 0,
};

async function load(): Promise<Dash> {
  const m = monthKey();
  const nw = await recordSnapshot();
  return {
    nw, snaps: await listSnapshots(6), totals: await monthTotals(m), spend: await spendingByCategory(m),
    budgets: await listBudgetLines(m), recent: await listTransactions({ limit: 10 }),
    pending: (await listPending()).length, 
    drafts: (await listDrafts()).length, 
    upcoming: await upcomingPayments(3),
    recurringTotal: await getTotalRecurring(), // NUEVO: Cargamos el total
  };
}

export default function Dashboard() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { width } = useWindowDimensions();
  const [d] = useFocusData(load, EMPTY);

  const prev = d.snaps.length >= 2 ? d.snaps[d.snaps.length - 2]! : null;
  const delta = prev ? d.nw.net - prev.net : null;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 14 }}>
        <AppText variant="title">Inicio</AppText>

        {d.drafts > 0 && (
          <Pressable onPress={() => router.push('/drafts')}>
            <Card style={{ borderColor: colors.primary }}>
              <AppText color={colors.primary}>ðŸ“¥ {d.drafts} notificaciÃ³n(es) por revisar</AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>El motor no las entendiÃ³. Toca para procesarlas.</AppText>
            </Card>
          </Pressable>
        )}

        {d.pending > 0 && (
          <Pressable onPress={() => router.push('/pending')}>
            <Card style={{ borderColor: colors.warning }}>
              <AppText color={colors.warning}>âš  {d.pending} compra(s) a crÃ©dito por completar</AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Toca para indicar en cuÃ¡ntas cuotas fueron.</AppText>
            </Card>
          </Pressable>
        )}

        <Card style={{ backgroundColor: colors.primary, borderColor: colors.primary }}>
          <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.onPrimary}>Patrimonio neto</AppText>
          <AppText variant="big" color={colors.onPrimary}>{formatCOP(d.nw.net)}</AppText>
          {delta !== null && (
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.onPrimary}>
              {delta >= 0 ? 'â–²' : 'â–¼'} {formatCOP(Math.abs(delta))} vs. mes anterior
            </AppText>
          )}
        </Card>

        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 12 }}>
          <Card style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Activos</AppText>
            <AppText color={colors.success}>{formatCOP(d.nw.assets)}</AppText>
          </Card>
          <Card style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Pasivos</AppText>
            <AppText color={colors.danger}>{formatCOP(d.nw.liabilities)}</AppText>
          </Card>
        </View>
        
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 12 }}>
          <Card style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Ingresos del mes</AppText>
            <AppText color={colors.success}>{formatCOP(d.totals.income)}</AppText>
          </Card>
          <Card style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Gastos del mes</AppText>
            <AppText color={colors.danger}>{formatCOP(d.totals.expense)}</AppText>
          </Card>
        </View>

        {/* NUEVO: Tarjeta de Gastos Fijos */}
        {d.recurringTotal > 0 && (
          <Card style={{ backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderColor: colors.border }}>
            <Pressable onPress={() => router.push('/recurring')} style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <View>
                <AppText variant="subtitle">Gastos fijos comprometidos</AppText>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Arriendo, servicios, suscripcionesâ€¦</AppText>
              </View>
              <AppText color={colors.warning}>{formatCOP(d.recurringTotal)}</AppText>
            </Pressable>
          </Card>
        )}

        {d.upcoming.length > 0 && (
          <Card>
            <AppText variant="subtitle">PrÃ³ximos pagos</AppText>
            {d.upcoming.map((u) => <InfoRow key={u.installmentId} label={`${u.dueDate} Â· ${u.debtName}`} value={formatCOP(u.total)} />)}
          </Card>
        )}

        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">Gastos por categorÃ­a</AppText>
          <DonutChart data={d.spend.map((s) => ({ label: s.name, value: s.total }))} />
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">EvoluciÃ³n del patrimonio</AppText>
          <LineChart points={d.snaps.map((s) => ({ label: s.month, value: s.net }))} width={Math.max(240, width - 40 - 34)} />
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
            <AppText variant="subtitle">Presupuestos</AppText>
            <Pressable onPress={() => router.push('/budgets')}><AppText color={colors.primary}>Configurar</AppText></Pressable>
          </View>
          {d.budgets.length === 0 && <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>AÃºn no tienes presupuestos.</AppText>}
          {d.budgets.map((b) => {
            const s = budgetStatus(b.spent, b.amount, b.threshold);
            return (
              <View key={b.categoryId} style={{ gap: 4 }}>
                <InfoRow label={b.name} value={`${formatCOP(b.spent)} / ${formatCOP(b.amount)}`} color={s.level === 'over' ? colors.danger : s.level === 'warn' ? colors.warning : undefined} />
                <ProgressBar ratio={s.ratio} level={s.level} />
                {s.level !== 'ok' && (
                  <AppText variant="caption" color={s.level === 'over' ? colors.danger : colors.warning}>
                    {s.level === 'over' ? `Te pasaste por ${formatCOP(-s.remaining)}` : `Llevas el ${Math.round(s.ratio * 100)} % del presupuesto`}
                  </AppText>
                )}
              </View>
            );
          })}
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">Ãšltimas transacciones</AppText>
          {d.recent.length === 0 && <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>TodavÃ­a no hay transacciones. Agrega la primera en la pestaÃ±a Movimientos.</AppText>}
          {d.recent.map((t) => (
            <InfoRow key={t.id} label={t.merchant ?? t.category_name ?? 'Sin descripciÃ³n'}
              value={`${t.type === 'income' ? '+' : '-'}${formatCOP(t.amount)}`} color={t.type === 'income' ? colors.success : colors.danger} />
          ))}
        </Card>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\app\(tabs)\settings.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import Constants from 'expo-constants';
import { useRouter, type Href } from 'expo-router';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { NotificationListener } from '../../modules/finzen-notifications';
import { AppText, Card, Chip, InfoRow } from '../../src/components/ui';
import { useFocusData } from '../../src/hooks/useFocusData';
import { getDb, isDbEncrypted } from '../../src/services/db';
import { SIMULATED_NOTIFICATIONS, ingestNotification } from '../../src/services/notifications';
import { isSignedIn, lastSyncAt, pendingCount } from '../../src/services/sync';
import { PALETTES, type ModePreference, type PaletteId } from '../../src/theme/palettes';
import { useTheme } from '../../src/theme/ThemeProvider';

const MODES: { id: ModePreference; label: string }[] = [
  { id: 'light', label: 'Claro' }, { id: 'dark', label: 'Oscuro' }, { id: 'auto', label: 'AutomÃ¡tico' },
];

async function diagnostics() {
  const db = await getDb();
  const q = (sql: string) => db.getFirstAsync<{ n: number }>(sql).then((r) => r?.n ?? 0);
  const last = await lastSyncAt();
  return {
    version: (await db.getFirstAsync<{ user_version: number }>('PRAGMA user_version'))?.user_version ?? 0,
    queue: await pendingCount(),
    tx: await q('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM transactions WHERE deleted_at IS NULL'),
    synced: await isSignedIn(),
    last,
    encrypted: isDbEncrypted(),
    listener: NotificationListener.available ? (NotificationListener.isEnabled() ? 'Activado' : 'Desactivado') : 'No disponible en esta versiÃ³n',
  };
}

export default function Settings() {
  const { colors, paletteId, mode, setPalette, setMode } = useTheme();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [diag, reloadDiag] = useFocusData(diagnostics, { version: 0, queue: 0, tx: 0, synced: false, last: null as number | null, listener: 'â€”', encrypted: true });
  const [simResult, setSimResult] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const link = (label: string, path: string, hint?: string) => (
    <Pressable key={path} onPress={() => router.push(path as Href)} style={{ paddingVertical: 8 }}>
      <AppText color={colors.primary}>{label} â€º</AppText>
      {hint ? <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>{hint}</AppText> : null}
    </Pressable>
  );

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 14 }}>
        <AppText variant="title">Ajustes</AppText>

        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">Paleta</AppText>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
            {(Object.keys(PALETTES) as PaletteId[]).map((id) => (
              <Chip key={id} label={PALETTES[id].label} selected={paletteId === id} onPress={() => setPalette(id)} />
            ))}
          </View>
        </Card>
        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">Modo</AppText>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
            {MODES.map((m) => <Chip key={m.id} label={m.label} selected={mode === m.id} onPress={() => setMode(m.id)} />)}
          </View>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">Captura automÃ¡tica</AppText>
          {link('Lectura de notificaciones', '/notification-sources', `Estado: ${diag.listener}. Elige quÃ© bancos y billeteras lee FinZen.`)}
          {link('Reglas de categorizaciÃ³n', '/rules')}
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">Finanzas</AppText>
          {link('Cuentas y activos', '/accounts')}
          {link('Presupuestos mensuales', '/budgets')}
          {link('Gastos fijos y suscripciones', '/recurring')} {/* <--- NUEVO */}
          {link('Escanear un recibo', '/scan')}
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">Seguridad y datos</AppText>
          {link('Bloqueo con huella y PIN', '/security')}
          {link('Exportar, importar y borrar datos', '/data')}
          {link('SincronizaciÃ³n en la nube (opcional)', '/sync', diag.synced ? 'Conectada' : 'Desconectada: tus datos solo estÃ¡n en este celular.')}
          {link('Configurar IA del asistente', '/assistant-settings')}
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">Probar notificaciones</AppText>
          <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>
            Simula una notificaciÃ³n para ver el flujo completo (filtro de seguridad â†’ compra pendiente â†’ cuotas) sin esperar una real.
          </AppText>
          {SIMULATED_NOTIFICATIONS.map((n) => (
            <Pressable
              key={n.label}
              onPress={async () => {
                const out = await ingestNotification({ packageName: n.packageName, text: n.text, receivedAt: Date.now() });
                setSimResult(`${n.label} â†’ ${out}`);
                reloadDiag();
              }}
              style={{ paddingVertical: 6 }}
            >
              <AppText color={colors.primary}>â–¶ {n.label}</AppText>
            </Pressable>
          ))}
          {simResult && <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>{simResult}</AppText>}
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">DiagnÃ³stico</AppText>
          <InfoRow label="VersiÃ³n de la app" value={Constants.expoConfig?.version ?? '-'} />
          <InfoRow label="VersiÃ³n de la base de datos" value={String(diag.version)} />
          <InfoRow label="Base de datos" value={diag.encrypted ? 'Cifrada (AES-256)' : 'âš  SIN CIFRAR'} color={diag.encrypted ? colors.success : colors.danger} />
          <InfoRow label="Transacciones" value={String(diag.tx)} />
          <InfoRow label="Lector de notificaciones" value={diag.listener} />
          <InfoRow label="SincronizaciÃ³n" value={diag.synced ? 'Conectada' : 'No configurada'} />
          {diag.synced && <InfoRow label="Cambios por subir" value={String(diag.queue)} />}
          {diag.last ? <InfoRow label="Ãšltima sincronizaciÃ³n" value={new Date(diag.last).toLocaleString('es-CO')} /> : null}
        </Card>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Alert, Pressable, ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { addMonthKey, monthKey } from '@finzen/core';
import { AppText, Card } from '../../src/components/ui';
import { useFocusData } from '../../src/hooks/useFocusData';
import { deleteTransaction, listTransactions, monthTotals, type MonthTotals, type TxRow } from '../../src/services/transactions';
import { useTheme } from '../../src/theme/ThemeProvider';
import { formatCOP } from '../../src/utils/format';

const MONTHS = ['enero', 'febrero', 'marzo', 'abril', 'mayo', 'junio', 'julio', 'agosto', 'septiembre', 'octubre', 'noviembre', 'diciembre'];
const label = (m: string) => `${MONTHS[Number(m.slice(5)) - 1]} ${m.slice(0, 4)}`;
const PAY_LABEL: Record<string, string> = { cash: 'Efectivo', debit: 'DÃ©bito', credit: 'Tarjeta', bnpl: 'Cuotas', unknown: '' };

export default function Transactions() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [month, setMonth] = useState(monthKey());
  const [data, reload] = useFocusData<{ rows: TxRow[]; totals: MonthTotals }>(
    async () => ({ rows: await listTransactions({ month }), totals: await monthTotals(month) }),
    { rows: [], totals: { income: 0, expense: 0, debtPayments: 0 } },
  );
  // Al cambiar de mes se recarga (useFocusData solo recarga al enfocar).
  React.useEffect(reload, [month, reload]);

  const remove = (t: TxRow) =>
    Alert.alert('Eliminar transacciÃ³n', `${t.merchant ?? 'Sin nombre'} Â· ${formatCOP(t.amount)}`, [
      { text: 'Cancelar', style: 'cancel' },
      {
        text: 'Eliminar', style: 'destructive',
        onPress: async () => {
          const ok = await deleteTransaction(t.id);
          if (!ok) Alert.alert('No se puede', 'Esta compra pertenece a un plan de cuotas. Elimina la deuda desde "Deudas".');
          reload();
        },
      },
    ]);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 12, paddingBottom: 100 }}>
        {/* ... cabecera y tarjetas de totales ... */}

        {data.rows.length === 0 && <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>Sin movimientos en este mes.</AppText>}
        {data.rows.map((t) => (
          <Pressable 
            key={t.id} 
            onLongPress={() => remove(t)} 
            onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: '/transaction/new', params: { id: t.id } })} // <-- NUEVO: Tocar para editar
          >
            <Card style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <View style={{ flexShrink: 1 }}>
                <AppText>{t.merchant ?? t.category_name ?? 'Sin descripciÃ³n'}</AppText>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>
                  {new Date(t.occurred_at).toLocaleDateString('es-CO')} Â· {t.category_name ?? 'Sin categorÃ­a'}{PAY_LABEL[t.payment_method] ? ` Â· ${PAY_LABEL[t.payment_method]}` : ''}
                </AppText>
              </View>
              <AppText color={t.type === 'income' ? colors.success : colors.danger}>
                {t.type === 'income' ? '+' : '-'}{formatCOP(t.amount)}
              </AppText>
            </Card>
          </Pressable>
        ))}
        <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Toca para editar Â· MantÃ©n presionada para eliminar.</AppText>
      </ScrollView>

      {/* ... botÃ³n flotante igual ... */}
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

type IconName = React.ComponentProps<typeof Ionicons>['name'];
const tab = (title: string, icon: IconName) => ({
  title,
  tabBarIcon: ({ color, size }: { color: import('react-native').ColorValue; size: number }) => <Ionicons name={icon} size={size} color={color} />,
});

export default function TabsLayout() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  return (
    <Tabs
      screenOptions={{
        headerShown: false,
        tabBarActiveTintColor: colors.primary,
        tabBarInactiveTintColor: colors.textSecondary,
        tabBarStyle: { backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderTopColor: colors.border },
      }}
    >
      <Tabs.Screen name="index" options={tab('Inicio', 'home')} />
      <Tabs.Screen name="transactions" options={tab('Movimientos', 'swap-vertical')} />
      <Tabs.Screen name="debts" options={tab('Deudas', 'card')} />
      <Tabs.Screen name="assistant" options={tab('Asistente', 'sparkles')} />
      <Tabs.Screen name="settings" options={tab('Ajustes', 'settings')} />
    </Tabs>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { AppText, Button, Chip, Field, MoneyField, ScreenHeader } from '../../src/components/ui';
import { DEBT_KIND_LABEL, createDebt, type DebtKind } from '../../src/services/debts';
import { useTheme } from '../../src/theme/ThemeProvider';
import { parseDigits } from '../../src/utils/format';

const KINDS = Object.keys(DEBT_KIND_LABEL) as DebtKind[];
const day = (t: string) => { const d = parseDigits(t); return d >= 1 && d <= 31 ? d : null; };

export default function NewDebt() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<DebtKind>('credit_card');
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [limit, setLimit] = useState('');
  const [balance, setBalance] = useState('');
  const [payment, setPayment] = useState('');
  const [rate, setRate] = useState('');
  const [cut, setCut] = useState('');
  const [due, setDue] = useState('');
  const isCard = kind === 'credit_card';

  const save = async () => {
    await createDebt({
      name: name.trim(), kind, balance: parseDigits(balance), monthlyPayment: parseDigits(payment),
      annualRatePct: Number(rate.replace(',', '.')) || 0, dueDay: day(due),
      cutDay: isCard ? day(cut) : null, creditLimit: isCard ? parseDigits(limit) || null : null,
    });
    router.back();
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 14 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
        <ScreenHeader title="Nueva deuda" />
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
          {KINDS.map((k) => <Chip key={k} label={DEBT_KIND_LABEL[k]} selected={kind === k} onPress={() => setKind(k)} />)}
        </View>
        <Field label="Nombre" value={name} onChangeText={setName} placeholder={isCard ? 'Ej. Nu, Bancolombia Visa' : 'Ej. CrÃ©dito moto'} />
        {isCard && <MoneyField label="Cupo total de la tarjeta (COP)" value={limit} onChange={setLimit} />}
        <MoneyField label={isCard ? 'Saldo que ya debes en la tarjeta hoy (COP)' : 'Lo que debes hoy (COP)'} value={balance} onChange={setBalance} />
        {!isCard && <MoneyField label="Cuota mensual (COP)" value={payment} onChange={setPayment} />}
        <Field label="Tasa efectiva anual % (opcional)" value={rate} onChangeText={setRate} keyboardType="decimal-pad" placeholder="Ej. 28.5" />
        {isCard && <Field label="DÃ­a de corte (1-31)" value={cut} onChangeText={(t) => setCut(t.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 2))} keyboardType="number-pad" />}
        <Field label={isCard ? 'DÃ­a lÃ­mite de pago (1-31)' : 'DÃ­a de pago (1-31)'} value={due} onChangeText={(t) => setDue(t.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 2))} keyboardType="number-pad" />
        {isCard && (
          <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>
            Si ya tienes compras diferidas a cuotas en esta tarjeta, agrÃ©galas despuÃ©s como "compra a cuotas" para que el calendario quede exacto.
          </AppText>
        )}
        <Button label="Guardar" onPress={save} disabled={!name.trim() || (isCard ? !parseDigits(limit) : !parseDigits(balance))} />
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

import {

  type Debt, type Installment,
} from '../../src/services/debts';
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Alert, Pressable, ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';

import { ProgressBar } from '../../src/components/charts';
import { AppText, Button, Card, Chip, Field, InfoRow, MoneyField, ScreenHeader } from '../../src/components/ui';
import { useFocusData } from '../../src/hooks/useFocusData';
import { listAccounts } from '../../src/services/repo';
import { useTheme } from '../../src/theme/ThemeProvider';
import { formatCOP, parseDigits } from '../../src/utils/format';

interface Detail {
  debt: Debt | null;
  installments: Installment[];
  plans: Debt[];
  card: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof cardSummary>> | null;
}

async function load(id: string): Promise<Detail> {
  const debt = await getDebt(id);
  if (!debt) return { debt: null, installments: [], plans: [], card: null };
  return {
    debt,
    installments: await getInstallments(id),
    plans: debt.kind === 'credit_card' ? await listPlansOf(id) : [],
    card: debt.kind === 'credit_card' ? await cardSummary(debt) : null,
  };
}

export default function DebtDetail() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const [d, reload] = useFocusData<Detail>(() => load(String(id)), { debt: null, installments: [], plans: [], card: null });
  const [accounts] = useFocusData(listAccounts, []);
  const [accountId, setAccountId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [payAmount, setPayAmount] = useState('');

  // Simulador "Â¿quÃ© pasa si abono extra?"
  const [simPayment, setSimPayment] = useState('');
  const [simExtra, setSimExtra] = useState('');
  const [simMode, setSimMode] = useState<'monthly' | 'oneTime'>('monthly');

  const debt = d.debt;
  const isCard = debt?.kind === 'credit_card';
  const basePayment = debt ? (parseDigits(simPayment) || Math.max(0, (debt.monthly_payment ?? 0) - debt.fixed_fee_per_installment)) : 0;
  const sim = useMemo(() => {
    const extra = parseDigits(simExtra);
    if (!debt || !extra || !basePayment || debt.balance <= 0) return null;
    return compareExtraPayment(debt.balance, debt.monthly_rate, basePayment, extra, simMode);
  }, [debt, simExtra, simMode, basePayment]);

  if (!debt) return null;
  const pendingInst = d.installments.filter((i) => !i.paid_at);
  const paidInst = d.installments.length - pendingInst.length;

  const confirmDelete = () =>
    Alert.alert('Eliminar deuda', 'Se eliminarÃ¡ con su calendario de cuotas (y los planes de la tarjeta, si es una tarjeta). Â¿Continuar?', [
      { text: 'Cancelar', style: 'cancel' },
      { text: 'Eliminar', style: 'destructive', onPress: async () => { await deleteDebt(debt.id); router.back(); } },
    ]);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 14 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
        <ScreenHeader title={debt.name} />
        <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>{DEBT_KIND_LABEL[debt.kind]}{debt.provider ? ` Â· ${debt.provider}` : ''}</AppText>

        {/* â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Tarjeta: cupo, corte y pago â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */}
        {isCard && d.card && (
          <Card>
            <ProgressBar ratio={d.card.utilization} level={d.card.utilization > 1 ? 'over' : d.card.utilization >= 0.8 ? 'warn' : 'ok'} />
            <InfoRow label="Cupo total" value={formatCOP(debt.credit_limit ?? 0)} />
            <InfoRow label="Cupo usado" value={formatCOP(d.card.used)} color={colors.danger} />
            <InfoRow label="Cupo disponible" value={formatCOP(d.card.available)} color={colors.success} />
            <InfoRow label="Saldo sin diferir" value={formatCOP(debt.balance)} />
            {d.card.nextCut && <InfoRow label="PrÃ³ximo corte" value={d.card.nextCut} />}
            {d.card.nextDue && <InfoRow label="PrÃ³ximo pago lÃ­mite" value={d.card.nextDue} />}
            {debt.monthly_rate > 0 && debt.balance > 0 && (
              <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.warning}>
                Si no pagas el saldo sin diferir completo, pagarÃ­as â‰ˆ {formatCOP(d.card.estimatedMonthlyInterest)} de interÃ©s este mes.
              </AppText>
            )}
          </Card>
        )}
        {!isCard && (
          <Card>
            <InfoRow label="Monto financiado" value={formatCOP(debt.principal)} />
            <InfoRow label="Saldo pendiente" value={formatCOP(debt.balance)} color={colors.danger} />
            {debt.monthly_payment ? <InfoRow label="Cuota mensual" value={formatCOP(debt.monthly_payment)} /> : null}
            {debt.monthly_rate > 0 && (
              <InfoRow label="Tasa" value={`${(debt.monthly_rate * 100).toFixed(2)} % mensual Â· ${(eaFromMonthlyRate(debt.monthly_rate) * 100).toFixed(1)} % E.A.`} />
            )}
            {debt.fixed_fee_per_installment > 0 && <InfoRow label="Cargo fijo por cuota" value={formatCOP(debt.fixed_fee_per_installment)} />}
            {debt.installments_total ? <InfoRow label="Progreso" value={`${paidInst} de ${debt.installments_total} cuotas pagadas`} /> : null}
            {debt.installments_total ? <ProgressBar ratio={paidInst / debt.installments_total} /> : null}
          </Card>
        )}

        {(isCard || pendingInst.length > 0) && accounts.length > 0 && (

            </View>

              onPress={async () => { await payCard(debt.id, parseDigits(payAmount), accountId); setPayAmount(''); reload(); }} />
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Las cuotas de compras diferidas se pagan desde cada compra, abajo.</AppText>
          </Card>
        )}

                {/* â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Abono a capital para deudas que NO son tarjetas â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */}
        {!isCard && debt.status === 'active' && (
          <Card>

              Reduce el saldo total de la deuda con un pago extra o libre.

            <MoneyField label="Monto del abono (COP)" value={payAmount} onChange={setPayAmount} />
            <Button 
              label="Registrar abono" 
              disabled={!parseDigits(payAmount) || debt.balance <= 0}
              onPress={async () => { 
                await payCapital(debt.id, parseDigits(payAmount), accountId); 
                setPayAmount(''); 
                reload(); 
              }} 
            />
          </Card>
        )}

        {/* â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Compras a cuotas de la tarjeta â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */}

                  </AppText>
                </Card>
              </Pressable>
            ))}
            <Button variant="ghost" label="Agregar compra a cuotas" onPress={() => router.push('/transaction/new')} />

ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\app\resolve\[id].tsx
==========================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { addMonthsClamped, nextOccurrence, todayString } from '@finzen/core';
import { InstallmentForm, type InstallmentChoice } from '../../src/components/InstallmentForm';
import { AppText, Button, Card, Chip, InfoRow, ScreenHeader } from '../../src/components/ui';
import { useFocusData } from '../../src/hooks/useFocusData';
import { listCards } from '../../src/services/debts';
import { listAccounts } from '../../src/services/repo';
import {
  deleteTransaction, getTransaction, resolvePendingSinglePayment, resolvePendingWithAccount, resolvePendingWithPlan,
} from '../../src/services/transactions';
import { useTheme } from '../../src/theme/ThemeProvider';
import { formatCOP } from '../../src/utils/format';

/**
 * Pantalla "Â¿en cuÃ¡ntas cuotas?" â€” se abre desde el aviso de Inicio cuando llegÃ³ una
 * notificaciÃ³n de compra a crÃ©dito (Addi, SistecrÃ©dito, tarjetaâ€¦).
 */
export default function ResolvePending() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const [tx] = useFocusData(() => getTransaction(String(id)), null);
  const [cards] = useFocusData(listCards, []);
  const [accounts] = useFocusData(listAccounts, []);
  const [cardId, setCardId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'installments' | 'single' | 'account'>('installments');

  if (!tx) return null;
  const isBnpl = tx.payment_method === 'bnpl';
  const effectiveCard = !isBnpl ? cards.find((c) => c.id === (cardId ?? cards[0]?.id)) ?? null : null;
  const suggested = /(\d+)\s+cuotas/.exec(tx.note ?? '')?.[1];
  const firstDue = effectiveCard?.due_day ? nextOccurrence(todayString(), effectiveCard.due_day) : addMonthsClamped(todayString(), 1);
  const done = () => router.back();

  const submitPlan = async (c: InstallmentChoice) => {
    await resolvePendingWithPlan(tx.id, {
      name: tx.merchant ?? 'Compra a cuotas',
      provider: isBnpl ? null : effectiveCard?.name ?? null,
      parentDebtId: effectiveCard?.id ?? null,
      installments: c.installments, mode: c.mode, firstDueDate: c.firstDueDate,
    });
    done();
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 14 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
        <ScreenHeader title="Compra a crÃ©dito" />
        <Card>
          <InfoRow label="Comercio" value={tx.merchant ?? 'Sin nombre'} />
          <InfoRow label="Monto" value={formatCOP(tx.amount)} color={colors.danger} />
          <InfoRow label="Fecha" value={new Date(tx.occurred_at).toLocaleDateString('es-CO')} />
        </Card>

        {!isBnpl && cards.length > 0 && (
          <>
            <AppText variant="subtitle">Â¿Con quÃ© tarjeta?</AppText>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
              {cards.map((c) => (
                <Chip key={c.id} label={c.name} selected={effectiveCard?.id === c.id} onPress={() => setCardId(c.id)} />
              ))}
            </View>
          </>
        )}
        {!isBnpl && cards.length === 0 && (
          <AppText color={colors.warning}>No tienes tarjetas registradas. Se crearÃ¡ como compra a cuotas suelta; puedes agregar tu tarjeta en Deudas.</AppText>
        )}

        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
          <Chip label="A cuotas" selected={mode === 'installments'} onPress={() => setMode('installments')} />
          {effectiveCard && <Chip label="A 1 pago (tarjeta)" selected={mode === 'single'} onPress={() => setMode('single')} />}
          <Chip label="No fue a crÃ©dito" selected={mode === 'account'} onPress={() => setMode('account')} />
        </View>

        {mode === 'installments' && (
          <InstallmentForm
            principal={tx.amount}
            defaultInstallments={suggested ? Number(suggested) : null}
            defaultFirstDue={firstDue}
            submitLabel="Confirmar cuotas"
            onSubmit={submitPlan}
          />
        )}
        {mode === 'single' && effectiveCard && (
          <Button label={`Sumar al saldo de ${effectiveCard.name}`} onPress={async () => { await resolvePendingSinglePayment(tx.id, effectiveCard.id); done(); }} />
        )}
        {mode === 'account' && (
          <View style={{ gap: 8 }}>
            <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>Elige la cuenta de la que saliÃ³:</AppText>
            {accounts.map((a) => (
              <Button key={a.id} variant="ghost" label={a.name} onPress={async () => { await resolvePendingWithAccount(tx.id, a.id); done(); }} />
            ))}
            {accounts.length === 0 && <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>No tienes cuentas registradas.</AppText>}
          </View>
        )}

        <Button variant="ghost" label="Descartar esta transacciÃ³n" onPress={async () => { await deleteTransaction(tx.id); done(); }} />
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\app\transaction\new.tsx
==========================================
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Alert, ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { addMonthsClamped, nextOccurrence, parseSpokenExpense, todayString } from '@finzen/core';
import { InstallmentForm } from '../../src/components/InstallmentForm';
import { VoiceButton } from '../../src/components/VoiceButton';
import { AppText, Button, Card, Chip, Field, MoneyField, ScreenHeader } from '../../src/components/ui';
import { useFocusData } from '../../src/hooks/useFocusData';
import { listCards } from '../../src/services/debts';
import { listAccounts } from '../../src/services/repo';
import {
  createInstallmentPurchase, createTransaction, updateTransaction, listCategories, suggestCategory, getTransaction, type TxType,
} from '../../src/services/transactions';
import { useTheme } from '../../src/theme/ThemeProvider';
import { formatCOP, groupDigits, parseDigits } from '../../src/utils/format';

type Pay = { kind: 'account'; id: string } | { kind: 'card'; id: string } | { kind: 'bnpl' } | null;

export default function NewTransaction() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  const router = useRouter();
  const rawParams = useLocalSearchParams<{ amount?: string; merchant?: string; date?: string; id?: string }>();
  
  // Validaciones bÃ¡sicas
  const params = {
    amount: /^\d{1,13}$/.test(String(rawParams.amount ?? '')) ? String(rawParams.amount) : undefined,
    merchant: rawParams.merchant ? String(rawParams.merchant).replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/g, ' ').slice(0, 60) : undefined,
    date: Number.isFinite(Number(rawParams.date)) && Number(rawParams.date) > 0 && Number(rawParams.date) < 4.1e12 ? String(rawParams.date) : undefined,
    id: rawParams.id ? String(rawParams.id) : undefined,
  };

  const isEditing = !!params.id;
  const [accounts] = useFocusData(listAccounts, []);
  const [cards] = useFocusData(listCards, []);
  const [categories] = useFocusData(listCategories, []);

  const [type, setType] = useState<TxType>('expense');
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(params.amount ? groupDigits(params.amount) : '');
  const [merchant, setMerchant] = useState(params.merchant ?? '');
  const [categoryId, setCategoryId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [userPickedCategory, setUserPickedCategory] = useState(false);
  const [pay, setPay] = useState<Pay>(null);
  const [provider, setProvider] = useState('');
  const [inInstallments, setInInstallments] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  // NUEVO: Cargar datos si estamos editando
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isEditing) return;
    getTransaction(params.id!).then(tx => {
      if (!tx) return;
      setType(tx.type);
      setAmount(groupDigits(String(tx.amount)));
      if (tx.merchant) setMerchant(tx.merchant);
      if (tx.category_id) { setCategoryId(tx.category_id); setUserPickedCategory(true); }
      if (tx.account_id) setPay({ kind: 'account', id: tx.account_id });
      else if (tx.debt_id) setPay({ kind: 'card', id: tx.debt_id });
    });
  }, [isEditing, params.id]);

  const value = parseDigits(amount);
  const usesCredit = pay?.kind === 'card' || pay?.kind === 'bnpl';
  const catById = useMemo(() => new Map(categories.map((c) => [c.id, c])), [categories]);
  const visibleCats = categories.filter((c) => c.kind === type);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (userPickedCategory || !merchant.trim() || type !== 'expense') return;
    let alive = true;
    suggestCategory(merchant).then((id) => { if (alive && id) setCategoryId(id); });
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [merchant, userPickedCategory, type]);

  const selectedCard = pay?.kind === 'card' ? cards.find((c) => c.id === pay.id) ?? null : null;
  const defaultFirstDue = selectedCard?.due_day
    ? nextOccurrence(todayString(), selectedCard.due_day)
    : addMonthsClamped(todayString(), 1);

  const baseTx = () => ({
    type, amount: value, merchant: merchant.trim() || null,
    categoryId: categoryId ?? (type === 'income' ? 'cat_ingreso_otros' : 'cat_otros'),
    occurredAt: params.date ? Number(params.date) : undefined,
  });

  const save = async () => {
    if (!value || saving) return;
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      let paymentMethod: any = 'cash';
      let accountId: string | null = null;
      let cardId: string | null = null;

      if (pay?.kind === 'card') { paymentMethod = 'credit'; cardId = pay.id; }
      else if (pay?.kind === 'account') { paymentMethod = 'debit'; accountId = pay.id; }

      if (isEditing) {
        // MODO EDICIÃ“N
        const ok = await updateTransaction(params.id!, { ...baseTx(), paymentMethod, accountId, cardId });
        if (!ok) Alert.alert('No se puede', 'Esta compra pertenece a un plan de cuotas. ElimÃ­nala desde "Deudas" si necesitas cambiarla.');
      } else {
        // MODO CREACIÃ“N
        await createTransaction({ ...baseTx(), paymentMethod, accountId, cardId });
      }
      router.back();
    } finally { setSaving(false); }
  };

  const saveInstallments = async (c: { installments: number; mode: any; firstDueDate: string }) => {
    if (!value || saving || !pay || isEditing) return; // No se permiten cuotas en ediciÃ³n directa
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const label = baseTx().merchant ?? 'Compra a cuotas';
      await createInstallmentPurchase(
        { ...baseTx(), paymentMethod: pay.kind === 'bnpl' ? 'bnpl' : 'credit' },
        {
          name: label, provider: pay.kind === 'bnpl' ? provider.trim() || null : selectedCard?.name ?? null,
          parentDebtId: pay.kind === 'card' ? pay.id : null,
          installments: c.installments, mode: c.mode, firstDueDate: c.firstDueDate,
        },
      );
      router.back();
    } finally { setSaving(false); }
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 14 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
        <ScreenHeader title={isEditing ? "Editar transacciÃ³n" : "Nueva transacciÃ³n"} />

        {!isEditing && (
          <>
            <VoiceButton onText={(t) => {
              const v = parseSpokenExpense(t);
              setType(v.type);
              if (v.amount) setAmount(groupDigits(String(v.amount)));
              if (v.merchant) { setMerchant(v.merchant); setUserPickedCategory(false); }
            }} />
            <Button variant="ghost" label="ðŸ“· Escanear un recibo" onPress={() => router.push('/scan')} />
          </>
        )}

        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
          <Chip label="Gasto" selected={type === 'expense'} onPress={() => { setType('expense'); setCategoryId(null); setUserPickedCategory(false); }} />
          <Chip label="Ingreso" selected={type === 'income'} onPress={() => { setType('income'); setCategoryId(null); setPay(null); setInInstallments(false); }} />
        </View>

        <MoneyField label="Monto (COP)" value={amount} onChange={setAmount} />
        <Field label={type === 'expense' ? 'Comercio o descripciÃ³n' : 'Origen del ingreso'} value={merchant} onChangeText={setMerchant} placeholder="Ej. Rappi, Falabella, Salario" />

        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">CategorÃ­a</AppText>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
            {visibleCats.map((c) => {
              const parent = c.parent_id ? catById.get(c.parent_id) : null;
              return (
                <Chip key={c.id} label={parent ? `${parent.name} â€º ${c.name}` : c.name} selected={categoryId === c.id}
                  onPress={() => { setCategoryId(c.id); setUserPickedCategory(true); }} />
              );
            })}
          </View>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">{type === 'expense' ? 'Â¿Con quÃ© pagaste?' : 'Â¿A quÃ© cuenta entrÃ³?'}</AppText>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
            {accounts.map((a) => (
              <Chip key={a.id} label={a.name} selected={pay?.kind === 'account' && pay.id === a.id}
                onPress={() => { setPay({ kind: 'account', id: a.id }); setInInstallments(false); }} />
            ))}
            {type === 'expense' && cards.map((c) => (
              <Chip key={c.id} label={`ðŸ’³ ${c.name}`} selected={pay?.kind === 'card' && pay.id === c.id}
                onPress={() => setPay({ kind: 'card', id: c.id })} />
            ))}
            {/* Ocultamos Addi/Cuotas si estamos editando para evitar descuadres de calendarios */}
            {type === 'expense' && !isEditing && (
              <Chip label="Addi / SistecrÃ©dito / otro" selected={pay?.kind === 'bnpl'} onPress={() => { setPay({ kind: 'bnpl' }); setInInstallments(true); }} />
            )}
          </View>
        </Card>

        {pay?.kind === 'bnpl' && (
          <Field label="Â¿CuÃ¡l? (Addi, SistecrÃ©ditoâ€¦)" value={provider} onChangeText={setProvider} />
        )}

        {usesCredit && pay?.kind === 'card' && !isEditing && (
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
            <Chip label="A 1 pago" selected={!inInstallments} onPress={() => setInInstallments(false)} />
            <Chip label="Diferido a cuotas" selected={inInstallments} onPress={() => setInInstallments(true)} />
          </View>
        )}

        {usesCredit && inInstallments && !isEditing ? (
          value > 0 ? (
            <Card>
              <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>Compra de {formatCOP(value)} a cuotas</AppText>
              <InstallmentForm principal={value} defaultFirstDue={defaultFirstDue} submitLabel="Guardar compra a cuotas" onSubmit={saveInstallments} />
            </Card>
          ) : <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>Escribe el monto para calcular las cuotas.</AppText>
        ) : (
          <Button label={isEditing ? "Guardar cambios" : "Guardar"} onPress={save} disabled={!value || saving} />
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\app\accounts.tsx

import { ACCOUNT_KIND_LABEL, addAccount, listAccounts, patchRow, softDelete, reconcileAccountBalance, type AccountKind } from '../src/services/repo';
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Alert, Pressable, ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { AppText, Button, Card, Chip, Field, MoneyField, ScreenHeader } from '../src/components/ui';
import { useFocusData } from '../src/hooks/useFocusData';
import { getDb } from '../src/services/db';
import { useTheme } from '../src/theme/ThemeProvider';
import { formatCOP, parseDigits } from '../src/utils/format';

const KINDS = Object.keys(ACCOUNT_KIND_LABEL) as AccountKind[];

export default function Accounts() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  const [items, reload] = useFocusData(listAccounts, []);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<AccountKind>('savings');
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState('');
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [newBalance, setNewBalance] = useState('');

  const total = items.reduce((s, a) => s + a.balance, 0);

  const saveBalance = async (id: string) => {
    const db = await getDb();
    // Usamos la nueva funciÃ³n que crea una transacciÃ³n de ajuste en el historial
    await db.withTransactionAsync(() => reconcileAccountBalance(db, id, parseDigits(newBalance)));
    setEditing(null); setNewBalance(''); reload();
  };

  const remove = (id: string, label: string) =>
    Alert.alert('Eliminar cuenta', `â€œ${label}â€ desaparecerÃ¡ de tu patrimonio. Los movimientos ya registrados se conservan.`, [
      { text: 'Cancelar', style: 'cancel' },
      { text: 'Eliminar', style: 'destructive', onPress: async () => { const db = await getDb(); await db.withTransactionAsync(() => softDelete(db, 'accounts', id)); reload(); } },
    ]);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 12 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
        <ScreenHeader title="Cuentas y activos" />
        <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>Total: {formatCOP(total)}</AppText>
        {items.map((a) => (
          <Card key={a.id}>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
              <View>
                <AppText>{a.name}</AppText>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>{ACCOUNT_KIND_LABEL[a.kind]}</AppText>
              </View>
              <AppText color={colors.success}>{formatCOP(a.balance)}</AppText>
            </View>
            {editing === a.id ? (
              <>
                <MoneyField label="Saldo real hoy (COP)" value={newBalance} onChange={setNewBalance} />
                <Button label="Guardar saldo" onPress={() => saveBalance(a.id)} />
              </>
            ) : (
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 16 }}>
                <Pressable onPress={() => { setEditing(a.id); setNewBalance(String(a.balance).replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, '.')); }}><AppText color={colors.primary}>Ajustar saldo</AppText></Pressable>
                <Pressable onPress={() => remove(a.id, a.name)}><AppText color={colors.danger}>Eliminar</AppText></Pressable>
              </View>
            )}
          </Card>
        ))}
        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">Agregar</AppText>
          <Field label="Nombre" value={name} onChangeText={setName} placeholder="Ej. Nequi, Bancolombia ahorros" />
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
            {KINDS.map((k) => <Chip key={k} label={ACCOUNT_KIND_LABEL[k]} selected={kind === k} onPress={() => setKind(k)} />)}
          </View>
          <MoneyField label="Saldo actual (COP)" value={amount} onChange={setAmount} />
          <Button label="Agregar cuenta" disabled={!name.trim()} onPress={async () => { await addAccount({ name: name.trim(), kind, balance: parseDigits(amount), currency: 'COP' }); setName(''); setAmount(''); reload(); }} />
        </Card>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { AppText, Button, Card, Chip, Field, ScreenHeader } from '../src/components/ui';
import {
  discoverGeminiModel, getAiConfig, getGeminiKey, saveAiConfig, saveGeminiKey, type AiConfig, type AiProvider,
} from '../src/services/ai';
import { useTheme } from '../src/theme/ThemeProvider';

export default function AssistantSettings() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  const [cfg, setCfg] = useState<AiConfig | null>(null);
  const [key, setKey] = useState('');
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [err, setErr] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => { getAiConfig().then(setCfg); getGeminiKey().then((k) => setKey(k ?? '')); }, []);
  if (!cfg) return null;
  const set = (p: Partial<AiConfig>) => { setCfg({ ...cfg, ...p }); setMsg(null); setErr(null); };

  const save = async () => {
    setErr(null);
    try {
      await saveGeminiKey(key);
      await saveAiConfig(cfg);
      if (cfg.provider === 'gemini' && key.trim()) {
        const model = cfg.geminiModel || (await discoverGeminiModel(key.trim()));
        setCfg({ ...cfg, geminiModel: model });
        await saveAiConfig({ ...cfg, geminiModel: model });
        setMsg(`âœ“ Guardado. ConexiÃ³n correcta con ${model}.`);
      } else setMsg('âœ“ Guardado.');
    } catch (e) { setErr(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)); }
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 14 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
        <ScreenHeader title="Configurar IA" />
        <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>
          La IA solo recibe totales anonimizados (sin nombres de cuentas, bancos ni comercios). Es opcional: el resto de la app no la necesita.
        </AppText>

        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
          {(['gemini', 'ollama'] as AiProvider[]).map((p) => (
            <Chip key={p} label={p === 'gemini' ? 'Gemini (nube, gratis)' : 'Ollama (tu PC)'} selected={cfg.provider === p} onPress={() => set({ provider: p })} />
          ))}
        </View>

        {cfg.provider === 'gemini' ? (
          <Card>
            <AppText variant="subtitle">Gemini</AppText>
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>
              1) Entra a aistudio.google.com con tu cuenta Google. 2) â€œGet API keyâ€ â†’ crea una clave gratis. 3) PÃ©gala aquÃ­. Se guarda cifrada en este celular.
              El nivel gratuito tiene lÃ­mites de uso y Google puede usar lo que envÃ­es para mejorar sus productos: por eso solo salen totales anonimizados.
            </AppText>
            <Field label="Clave de API" value={key} onChangeText={setKey} autoCapitalize="none" secureTextEntry placeholder="AIzaâ€¦" />
            <Field label="Modelo (vacÃ­o = elegir el Flash mÃ¡s reciente)" value={cfg.geminiModel} onChangeText={(t) => set({ geminiModel: t })} autoCapitalize="none" />
          </Card>
        ) : (
          <Card>
            <AppText variant="subtitle">Ollama (opcional)</AppText>
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>
              Solo si quieres una IA 100 % privada en tu PC. La PC debe estar encendida, en la misma red WiFi, con Ollama escuchando en la red
              (variable OLLAMA_HOST=0.0.0.0). Ejemplo de direcciÃ³n: http://192.168.1.20:11434
            </AppText>
            <Field label="DirecciÃ³n de Ollama" value={cfg.ollamaUrl} onChangeText={(t) => set({ ollamaUrl: t })} autoCapitalize="none" placeholder="http://192.168.1.20:11434" />
            <Field label="Modelo" value={cfg.ollamaModel} onChangeText={(t) => set({ ollamaModel: t })} autoCapitalize="none" />
          </Card>
        )}

        <Button label="Guardar y probar" onPress={save} />
        {msg && <AppText color={colors.success}>{msg}</AppText>}
        {err && <AppText color={colors.danger}>{err}</AppText>}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\app\budgets.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { AppText, Button, Card, MoneyField, ScreenHeader } from '../src/components/ui';
import { budgetAmounts, listCategories, setBudget } from '../src/services/transactions';
import { useTheme } from '../src/theme/ThemeProvider';
import { groupDigits, parseDigits } from '../src/utils/format';

/** Presupuesto mensual por categorÃ­a de primer nivel. 0 o vacÃ­o = sin presupuesto. */
export default function Budgets() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  const [cats, setCats] = useState<{ id: string; name: string }[]>([]);
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const all = (await listCategories()).filter((c) => !c.parent_id && c.kind === 'expense' && c.id !== 'cat_deudas');
      const amounts = await budgetAmounts();
      setCats(all);
      setValues(Object.fromEntries(all.map((c) => [c.id, amounts.get(c.id) ? groupDigits(String(amounts.get(c.id))) : ''])));
    })();
  }, []);

  const save = async () => {
    for (const c of cats) await setBudget(c.id, parseDigits(values[c.id] ?? ''));
    setSaved(true);
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 12 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
        <ScreenHeader title="Presupuestos" />
        <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>CuÃ¡nto quieres gastar al mes en cada categorÃ­a. Te aviso en el Inicio al llegar al 80 %.</AppText>
        <Card>
          {cats.map((c) => (
            <View key={c.id}>
              <MoneyField label={c.name} value={values[c.id] ?? ''} onChange={(v) => { setSaved(false); setValues((s) => ({ ...s, [c.id]: v })); }} />
            </View>
          ))}
        </Card>
        <Button label={saved ? 'âœ“ Guardado' : 'Guardar presupuestos'} onPress={save} />
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Alert, ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import * as DocumentPicker from 'expo-document-picker';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import type { ColumnMap } from '@finzen/core';
import { useLock } from '../src/components/LockProvider';
import { AppText, Button, Card, Chip, Field, ScreenHeader } from '../src/components/ui';
import {
  exportBackupJson, exportTransactionsCsv, importCsvRows, previewCsv, readPickedFile, restoreBackup, wipeAllData, type CsvPreview,
} from '../src/services/backup';
import { useTheme } from '../src/theme/ThemeProvider';

export default function DataScreen() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { updatePrefs } = useLock();
  const [pass, setPass] = useState('');
  const [pass2, setPass2] = useState('');
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<{ text: string; ok: boolean } | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [csv, setCsv] = useState<CsvPreview | null>(null);
  const [positiveIncome, setPositiveIncome] = useState<boolean | undefined>(undefined);

  const run = async (fn: () => Promise<string | void>) => {
    setBusy(true); setMsg(null);
    try { const t = await fn(); if (t) setMsg({ text: t, ok: true }); }
    catch (e) { setMsg({ text: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e), ok: false }); }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  };

  const pick = async (types: string[]) => {
    const r = await DocumentPicker.getDocumentAsync({ type: types, copyToCacheDirectory: true });
    return r.canceled ? null : await readPickedFile(r.assets[0]!.uri);
  };

  const importCsv = () => run(async () => {
    const text = await pick(['text/csv', 'text/comma-separated-values', 'text/plain', 'application/vnd.ms-excel', '*/*']);
    if (text === null) return;
    const p = previewCsv(text);
    if (!p.map) throw new Error('No encontrÃ© columnas de fecha y descripciÃ³n. Revisa que la primera fila sea el encabezado (Fecha, DescripciÃ³n, Valorâ€¦).');
    setCsv(p);
  });

  const confirmImport = (map: ColumnMap) => run(async () => {
    const r = await importCsvRows(csv!.rows, map, positiveIncome);
    setCsv(null);
    return `âœ“ Importadas ${r.imported}. Repetidas: ${r.duplicates}. Omitidas por formato: ${r.skipped}.`;
  });

  const restore = () => run(async () => {
    const text = await pick(['application/json', 'text/plain', '*/*']);
    if (text === null) return;
    // Si el respaldo estÃ¡ cifrado, la contraseÃ±a se escribe en el campo de abajo ANTES de elegir el archivo.
    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) =>
      Alert.alert('Restaurar copia', 'Esto REEMPLAZA todos los datos actuales por los de la copia. Â¿Continuar?', [
        { text: 'Cancelar', style: 'cancel', onPress: () => reject(new Error('RestauraciÃ³n cancelada.')) },
        { text: 'Restaurar', style: 'destructive', onPress: () => resolve() },
      ]),
    );
    const n = await restoreBackup(text, pass || undefined);
    return `âœ“ Restauradas ${n} filas. Reinicia la app para ver todo actualizado.`;
  });

  const wipe = () =>
    Alert.alert('Eliminar todos mis datos', 'Se borran cuentas, deudas, movimientos, presupuestos y ajustes de ESTE celular. No se puede deshacer. Si usas Supabase, bÃ³rralos tambiÃ©n desde SincronizaciÃ³n.', [
      { text: 'Cancelar', style: 'cancel' },
      { text: 'Eliminar todo', style: 'destructive', onPress: () => Alert.alert('Â¿Seguro?', 'Ãšltima confirmaciÃ³n.', [
        { text: 'No', style: 'cancel' },
        { text: 'SÃ­, eliminar', style: 'destructive', onPress: () => run(async () => { await updatePrefs({ enabled: false }); await wipeAllData(); router.replace('/'); }) },
      ]) },
    ]);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 14 }}>
        <ScreenHeader title="Mis datos" />
        <AppText color={colors.textSecondary}>Tus datos viven en este celular. AquÃ­ los sacas, los traes o los borras.</AppText>

        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">Exportar</AppText>
          <Button variant="ghost" label="Movimientos en CSV (Excel)" disabled={busy} onPress={() => run(async () => `âœ“ ${await exportTransactionsCsv()} movimientos exportados.`)} />
          <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>El CSV sale SIN cifrar (es para abrirlo en Excel): no lo dejes en lugares pÃºblicos.</AppText>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">Copia de seguridad cifrada</AppText>
          <Field label="ContraseÃ±a del respaldo (mÃ­nimo 10 caracteres)" value={pass} onChangeText={setPass} secureTextEntry autoCapitalize="none" />
          <Field label="Repite la contraseÃ±a (solo al crear)" value={pass2} onChangeText={setPass2} secureTextEntry autoCapitalize="none" />
          <Button variant="ghost" label="Crear copia de seguridad cifrada"
            disabled={busy || pass.length < 10 || pass !== pass2}
            onPress={() => run(async () => { await exportBackupJson(pass); return 'âœ“ Copia creada. Guarda la contraseÃ±a: sin ella NADIE puede abrirla (ni yo).'; })} />
          <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Se cifra con AES-256 antes de salir de la app. Luego se abre el menÃº de compartir: guÃ¡rdala en Drive o envÃ­ala a tu PC.</AppText>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <AppText variant="subtitle">Importar</AppText>
          <Button variant="ghost" label="Extracto bancario (CSV)" disabled={busy} onPress={importCsv} />
          <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Para restaurar una copia cifrada, escribe primero su contraseÃ±a en el campo de arriba.</AppText>
          <Button variant="ghost" label="Restaurar copia de seguridad" disabled={busy} onPress={restore} />
        </Card>

        {csv?.map && (
          <Card>
            <AppText variant="subtitle">Vista previa</AppText>
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>
              {csv.rows.length} filas. Fecha: â€œ{csv.header[csv.map.date]}â€ Â· DescripciÃ³n: â€œ{csv.header[csv.map.description]}â€ Â·
              {csv.map.debit !== null ? ` DÃ©bito: â€œ${csv.header[csv.map.debit]}â€ Â· CrÃ©dito: â€œ${csv.header[csv.map.credit!]}â€` : ` Monto: â€œ${csv.header[csv.map.amount ?? 0]}â€`}
            </AppText>
            {csv.rows.slice(0, 3).map((r, i) => <AppText key={i} variant="caption">{r.join(' | ').slice(0, 90)}</AppText>)}
            {csv.map.debit === null && (
              <>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Â¿CÃ³mo vienen los montos positivos?</AppText>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                  <Chip label="AutomÃ¡tico" selected={positiveIncome === undefined} onPress={() => setPositiveIncome(undefined)} />
                  <Chip label="Positivo = ingreso" selected={positiveIncome === true} onPress={() => setPositiveIncome(true)} />
                  <Chip label="Positivo = gasto" selected={positiveIncome === false} onPress={() => setPositiveIncome(false)} />
                </View>
              </>
            )}
            <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>Se importan como historial: no cambian los saldos de tus cuentas, y volver a importar el mismo archivo no duplica nada.</AppText>
            <Button label="Importar" disabled={busy} onPress={() => confirmImport(csv.map!)} />
            <Button variant="ghost" label="Cancelar" onPress={() => setCsv(null)} />
          </Card>
        )}

        <Card style={{ borderColor: colors.danger }}>
          <AppText variant="subtitle" color={colors.danger}>Zona de peligro</AppText>
          <Button variant="ghost" label="Eliminar todos mis datos" onPress={wipe} />
        </Card>

        {msg && <AppText color={msg.ok ? colors.success : colors.danger}>{msg.text}</AppText>}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { Alert, Pressable, ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { AppText, Button, Card, ScreenHeader } from '../src/components/ui';
import { useFocusData } from '../src/hooks/useFocusData';
import { deleteDraft, listDrafts, type DraftNotification } from '../src/services/notifications';
import { useTheme } from '../src/theme/ThemeProvider';

export default function DraftsScreen() {
  const { colors } = useTheme();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [drafts, reload] = useFocusData(listDrafts, []);

  const handleProcess = (draft: DraftNotification) => {
    // Enviamos el texto como "merchant" para que el formulario lo precargue.
    // (El formulario cortarÃ¡ el texto a 60 caracteres por seguridad, pero tÃº podrÃ¡s editarlo).
    router.push({
      pathname: '/transaction/new',
      params: { merchant: draft.masked_text },
    });
    // Nota: No borramos el borrador aquÃ­ para que no se pierda si el usuario cancela el formulario.
    // Lo ideal es borrarlo cuando se guarde la transacciÃ³n, pero por simplicidad
    // el usuario puede volver y eliminarlo manualmente si ya lo registrÃ³.
  };

  const handleDelete = (id: string) => {
    Alert.alert('Descartar notificaciÃ³n', 'Â¿Seguro que quieres eliminarla? No se registrarÃ¡ ningÃºn movimiento.', [
      { text: 'Cancelar', style: 'cancel' },
      {
        text: 'Eliminar',
        style: 'destructive',
        onPress: async () => {
          await deleteDraft(id);
          reload();
        },
      },
    ]);
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 12 }}>
        <ScreenHeader title="Notificaciones por revisar" />
        <AppText color={colors.textSecondary} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
          FinZen recibiÃ³ estas notificaciones, pero el formato no fue reconocido automÃ¡ticamente. Toca "Procesar" para registrar el movimiento.
        </AppText>

        {drafts.length === 0 && (
          <Card>
            <AppText color={colors.success}>âœ… Todo al dÃ­a. No hay notificaciones pendientes por revisar.</AppText>
          </Card>
        )}

        {drafts.map((d) => (
          <Card key={d.id}>
            <View style={{ gap: 6 }}>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.primary}>
                De: {d.source_package}
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={colors.textSecondary}>
                Recibida: {new Date(d.received_at).toLocaleString('es-CO')}
              </AppText>
              <Card style={{ backgroundColor: colors.surface, padding: 10, marginTop: 4 }}>
                <AppText style={{ fontStyle: 'italic' }}>"{d.masked_text}"</AppText>
              </Card>
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10, marginTop: 8 }}>
                <Button variant="ghost" label="Descartar ðŸ—‘ï¸" onPress={() => handleDelete(d.id)} />
                <Button label="Procesar âž¡ï¸" onPress={() => handleProcess(d)} />
              </View>
            </View>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Redirect } from 'expo-router';
import { getSetting } from '../src/services/repo';

/** Punto de entrada: primera vez â†’ onboarding; despuÃ©s â†’ pantalla principal. */
export default function Index() {
  const [target, setTarget] = useState<'/onboarding' | '/(tabs)' | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    getSetting('onboarded').then((v) => setTarget(v === '1' ? '/(tabs)' : '/onboarding'));
  }, []);
  return target ? <Redirect href={target} /> : null;
}
