import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import {
  aiSystemPrompt, buildAiContext, buildUserTurn, monthKey, type AiDebt, type AiSnapshot, type ChatMessage,
} from '@finzen/core';
import { cardSummary, listDebts, listPlansOf } from './debts';
import { getNetWorth, getSetting, setSetting } from './repo';
import { listBudgetLines, monthTotals, spendingByCategory } from './transactions';

/**
 * Asistente de IA. Dos proveedores, ambos opcionales:
 *  - Gemini (API gratuita de Google AI Studio): funciona desde el celular en cualquier parte.
 *  - Ollama: IA local en tu PC (necesita la PC encendida y en la misma red). El Ãºnico uso de la PC.
 * A cualquiera de los dos solo se le envÃ­an totales anonimizados (ver @finzen/core/ai.ts).
 * La clave de Gemini vive en SecureStore (Keystore de Android), nunca en el repo ni en la nube.
 */
export type AiProvider = 'gemini' | 'ollama';
export interface AiConfig {
  provider: AiProvider;
  geminiModel: string; // '' = elegir automÃ¡ticamente
  ollamaUrl: string;
  ollamaModel: string;
}

const GEMINI_KEY = 'finzen_gemini_key';

export async function getAiConfig(): Promise<AiConfig> {
  const [provider, geminiModel, ollamaUrl, ollamaModel] = await Promise.all(
    ['ai_provider', 'ai_gemini_model', 'ai_ollama_url', 'ai_ollama_model'].map(getSetting),
  );
  return {
    provider: provider === 'ollama' ? 'ollama' : 'gemini',
    geminiModel: geminiModel ?? '',
    ollamaUrl: ollamaUrl ?? '',
    ollamaModel: ollamaModel ?? 'llama3.2',
  };
}

/**
 * Ollama solo por HTTPS: el trÃ¡fico HTTP en claro estÃ¡ bloqueado en toda la app (asÃ­ nadie en tu WiFi
 * puede leer lo que se envÃ­a). Para tu PC usa un tÃºnel/proxy con HTTPS (p. ej. Tailscale Serve o Caddy).
 */
function assertSafeOllamaUrl(url: string) {
  if (url && !/^https:\/\/[^\s/]+/i.test(url)) {
    throw new Error('Por seguridad la direcciÃ³n de Ollama debe empezar por https:// (el trÃ¡fico sin cifrar estÃ¡ bloqueado).');
  }
}

export async function saveAiConfig(c: AiConfig): Promise<void> {
  assertSafeOllamaUrl(c.ollamaUrl.trim());
  await setSetting('ai_provider', c.provider, 'device');
  await setSetting('ai_gemini_model', c.geminiModel.trim(), 'device');
  await setSetting('ai_ollama_url', c.ollamaUrl.trim().replace(/\/+$/, ''), 'device');
  await setSetting('ai_ollama_model', c.ollamaModel.trim() || 'llama3.2', 'device');
}

export const clearGeminiKey = () => SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(GEMINI_KEY);
export const getGeminiKey = () => SecureStore.getItemAsync(GEMINI_KEY);
export const saveGeminiKey = (k: string) => (k.trim() ? SecureStore.setItemAsync(GEMINI_KEY, k.trim()) : SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(GEMINI_KEY));

export async function isAiConfigured(): Promise<boolean> {
  const c = await getAiConfig();
  return c.provider === 'ollama' ? c.ollamaUrl !== '' : !!(await getGeminiKey());
}

/** Foto de la situaciÃ³n financiera SIN nombres (solo tipos de deuda y totales). */
export async function buildSnapshot(): Promise<AiSnapshot> {
  const month = monthKey();
  const nw = await getNetWorth();
  const t = await monthTotals(month);
  const debts: AiDebt[] = [];
  for (const d of await listDebts()) {
    if (d.kind === 'credit_card') {
      const s = await cardSummary(d);
      debts.push({ kind: 'credit_card', balance: d.balance, monthlyRatePct: d.monthly_rate * 100, monthlyPayment: null, utilizationPct: s.utilization * 100 });
      for (const p of await listPlansOf(d.id)) {
        if (p.status === 'active') debts.push({ kind: 'bnpl', balance: p.balance, monthlyRatePct: p.monthly_rate * 100, monthlyPayment: p.monthly_payment });
      }
    } else {
      debts.push({ kind: d.kind, balance: d.balance, monthlyRatePct: d.monthly_rate * 100, monthlyPayment: d.monthly_payment });
    }
  }
  return {
    month, assets: nw.assets, liabilities: nw.liabilities, income: t.income, expense: t.expense, debtPayments: t.debtPayments,
    byCategory: (await spendingByCategory(month)).map((c) => ({ name: c.name, total: c.total })),
    budgets: (await listBudgetLines(month)).map((b) => ({ name: b.name, amount: b.amount, spent: b.spent })),
    debts,
  };
}

async function timedFetch(url: string, init: RequestInit, ms = 60_000): Promise<Response> {
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), ms);
  try { return await fetch(url, { ...init, signal: ctrl.signal }); }
  catch (e) {
    if ((e as Error).name === 'AbortError') throw new Error('La IA tardÃ³ demasiado en responder.');
    throw new Error('No se pudo conectar. Revisa tu internet (o que la PC con Ollama estÃ© encendida y en la misma red).');
  }
  finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
}

/** Elige el modelo "flash" mÃ¡s reciente disponible para la clave (los nombres cambian con el tiempo). */
export async function discoverGeminiModel(key: string): Promise<string> {
  const res = await timedFetch('https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?pageSize=100', { headers: { 'x-goog-api-key': key } }, 20_000);
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(await geminiError(res));
  const data = (await res.json()) as { models?: { name: string; supportedGenerationMethods?: string[] }[] };
  const candidates = (data.models ?? [])
    .filter((m) => m.supportedGenerationMethods?.includes('generateContent'))
    .map((m) => m.name.replace(/^models\//, ''))
    .filter((n) => /^gemini-[\d.]+-flash$/.test(n)) // estable, sin lite/preview/exp/imagen
    .sort((a, b) => parseFloat(b.split('-')[1]!) - parseFloat(a.split('-')[1]!));
  if (!candidates[0]) throw new Error('No encontrÃ© un modelo Gemini Flash disponible para tu clave.');
  return candidates[0];
}

async function geminiError(res: Response): Promise<string> {
  try {
    const j = (await res.json()) as { error?: { message?: string } };
    return `Gemini respondiÃ³ ${res.status}: ${j.error?.message ?? 'error'}`;
  } catch { return `Gemini respondiÃ³ ${res.status}`; }
}

async function askGemini(cfg: AiConfig, history: ChatMessage[], turn: string): Promise<string> {
  const key = await getGeminiKey();
  if (!key) throw new Error('Falta la clave de Gemini (Asistente â†’ âš™).');
  let model = cfg.geminiModel;
  if (!model) {
    model = await discoverGeminiModel(key);
    await setSetting('ai_gemini_model', model, 'device');
  }
  const contents = [
    ...history.map((m) => ({ role: m.role === 'user' ? 'user' : 'model', parts: [{ text: m.content }] })),
    { role: 'user', parts: [{ text: turn }] },
  ];
  const res = await timedFetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${encodeURIComponent(model)}:generateContent`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-goog-api-key': key },
    body: JSON.stringify({ systemInstruction: { parts: [{ text: aiSystemPrompt() }] }, contents, generationConfig: { temperature: 0.4 } }),
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(await geminiError(res));
  const j = (await res.json()) as { candidates?: { content?: { parts?: { text?: string }[] } }[] };
  const text = j.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.map((p) => p.text ?? '').join('').trim();
  if (!text) throw new Error('La IA no devolviÃ³ respuesta (puede haber sido bloqueada). Reformula la pregunta.');
  return text;
}

async function askOllama(cfg: AiConfig, history: ChatMessage[], turn: string): Promise<string> {
  if (!cfg.ollamaUrl) throw new Error('Falta la direcciÃ³n de Ollama (Asistente â†’ âš™).');
  const res = await timedFetch(`${cfg.ollamaUrl}/api/chat`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      model: cfg.ollamaModel, stream: false,
      messages: [{ role: 'system', content: aiSystemPrompt() }, ...history, { role: 'user', content: turn }],
    }),
  }, 120_000);
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Ollama respondiÃ³ ${res.status}. Â¿Descargaste el modelo "${cfg.ollamaModel}"? (ollama pull ${cfg.ollamaModel})`);
  const j = (await res.json()) as { message?: { content?: string } };
  const text = j.message?.content?.trim();
  if (!text) throw new Error('Ollama no devolviÃ³ respuesta.');
  return text;
}

/** Responde una pregunta con el contexto financiero anonimizado + los Ãºltimos mensajes del chat. */
export async function askAssistant(question: string, history: ChatMessage[]): Promise<string> {
  const cfg = await getAiConfig();
  const turn = buildUserTurn(buildAiContext(await buildSnapshot()), question);
  const recent = history.slice(-6);
  return cfg.provider === 'ollama' ? askOllama(cfg, recent, turn) : askGemini(cfg, recent, turn);
}

/**
 * Exportar / importar / restaurar / borrar. Todo local: el usuario es dueÃ±o de sus datos.
 * Reglas de seguridad de este archivo:
 *  - El respaldo JSON SIEMPRE sale cifrado (AES-256-GCM con contraseÃ±a). El CSV es para Excel y sale en claro:
 *    la pantalla lo avisa.
 *  - Los archivos temporales (exportaciones, extractos elegidos, fotos de recibos) se borran apenas se usan.
 *  - Un respaldo a restaurar no es de confianza: se valida tamaÃ±o, forma de las filas y se filtran los ajustes.
 */
const USER_TABLES = [
  // Orden de inserciÃ³n (padres antes que hijos)
  'categories', 'accounts', 'debts', 'debt_installments', 'transactions', 'budgets', 'categorization_rules',
] as const;
const BACKUP_VERSION = 1;
const MAX_IMPORT_BYTES = 5_000_000; // extractos CSV
const MAX_CSV_ROWS = 50_000;

// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Archivos temporales â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export function deleteQuietly(uri: string | undefined | null): void {
  if (!uri) return;
  try { const f = new File(uri); if (f.exists) f.delete(); } catch { /* ya no existe */ }
}

/** Borra restos de exportaciones, selector de archivos y cÃ¡mara. Se llama al abrir la app. */
export function cleanTempFiles(): void {
  try {
    for (const entry of new Directory(Paths.cache).list()) {
      const name = entry.name ?? '';
      if (/^finzen-/.test(name) || name === 'DocumentPicker' || name === 'ImagePicker' || name === 'Camera') {
        try { entry.delete(); } catch { /* en uso */ }
      }
    }
  } catch { /* sin acceso al cachÃ©: nada que limpiar */ }
}

async function shareText(filename: string, content: string, mime: string) {
  const file = new File(Paths.cache, filename);
  if (file.exists) file.delete();
  file.create();
  file.write(content);
  if (await Sharing.isAvailableAsync()) await Sharing.shareAsync(file.uri, { mimeType: mime, dialogTitle: filename });
  else throw new Error('Este dispositivo no permite compartir archivos.');
  // El menÃº de compartir ya tomÃ³ su copia; no dejamos el archivo en claro en el cachÃ©.
  setTimeout(() => deleteQuietly(file.uri), 120_000);
}

export async function exportTransactionsCsv(): Promise<number> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const rows = await db.getAllAsync<{
    occurred_at: number; merchant: string | null; cat: string | null; type: string; amount: number; payment_method: string; note: string | null;
  }>(
    `SELECT t.occurred_at, t.merchant, c.name AS cat, t.type, t.amount, t.payment_method, t.note
       FROM transactions t LEFT JOIN categories c ON c.id=t.category_id
      WHERE t.deleted_at IS NULL AND t.status='posted' ORDER BY t.occurred_at DESC`,
  );
  const head = ['Fecha', 'DescripciÃ³n', 'CategorÃ­a', 'Tipo', 'Monto', 'MÃ©todo', 'Nota'];
  // csvCell neutraliza fÃ³rmulas (=, +, -, @) para evitar inyecciÃ³n de fÃ³rmulas al abrir en Excel.
  const lines = rows.map((r) =>
    [new Date(r.occurred_at).toISOString().slice(0, 10), r.merchant, r.cat, r.type === 'income' ? 'Ingreso' : 'Gasto', r.amount, r.payment_method, r.note]
      .map(csvCell).join(','),
  );
  await shareText(`finzen-movimientos-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`, 'ï»¿' + [head.join(','), ...lines].join('\n'), 'text/csv');
  return rows.length;
}

/** Copia de seguridad completa, CIFRADA con contraseÃ±a. */
export async function exportBackupJson(passphrase: string): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const tables: Record<string, unknown[]> = {};
  for (const t of USER_TABLES) tables[t] = await db.getAllAsync(`SELECT * FROM ${t}`);
  tables.settings = filterRestoredSettings(await db.getAllAsync("SELECT * FROM settings WHERE scope='user'"));
  tables.net_worth_snapshots = await db.getAllAsync('SELECT * FROM net_worth_snapshots');
  const plain = JSON.stringify({ app: 'finzen', version: BACKUP_VERSION, exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(), tables });
  const enc = await encryptBackup(plain, passphrase, (n) => Crypto.getRandomBytes(n));
  await shareText(`finzen-respaldo-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.finzen`, enc, 'application/json');
}

/** Restaura una copia REEMPLAZANDO los datos actuales. Valida todo antes de borrar nada. */
export async function restoreBackup(fileText: string, passphrase?: string): Promise<number> {
  let text = fileText;
  if (isEncryptedBackup(fileText)) {
    if (!passphrase) throw new Error('Este respaldo estÃ¡ cifrado: escribe su contraseÃ±a.');
    text = await decryptBackup(fileText, passphrase);
  }
  let data: { app?: string; version?: number; tables?: Record<string, unknown[]> };
  try { data = JSON.parse(text); } catch { throw new Error('El archivo no es un respaldo vÃ¡lido.'); }
  if (data.app !== 'finzen' || !data.tables || typeof data.tables !== 'object') throw new Error('El archivo no es un respaldo de FinZen.');
  if ((data.version ?? 0) > BACKUP_VERSION) throw new Error('El respaldo es de una versiÃ³n mÃ¡s nueva de la app.');
  assertBackupSize(text, data.tables);

  const db = await getDb();
  let total = 0;
  await db.withTransactionAsync(async () => {
    await db.execAsync('PRAGMA defer_foreign_keys = ON');
    for (const t of [...USER_TABLES].reverse()) await db.runAsync(`DELETE FROM ${t}`);
    await db.runAsync('DELETE FROM net_worth_snapshots');
    await db.runAsync("DELETE FROM settings WHERE scope='user'");
    await db.runAsync('DELETE FROM sync_queue');

    for (const t of [...USER_TABLES, 'net_worth_snapshots'] as string[]) {
      const cols = new Set((await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(`PRAGMA table_info(${t})`)).map((c) => c.name));
      for (const row of data.tables![t] ?? []) {
        if (!isPlainRow(row)) throw new Error(`Fila invÃ¡lida en "${t}": el respaldo estÃ¡ daÃ±ado.`);
        const keys = Object.keys(row).filter((k) => cols.has(k)); // nombres de columna solo si existen en el esquema
        if (!keys.length) continue;
        await db.runAsync(
          `INSERT OR REPLACE INTO ${t} (${keys.join(',')}) VALUES (${keys.map(() => '?').join(',')})`,
          keys.map((k) => row[k] as string | number | null),
        );
        total++;
      }
    }
    // Ajustes: solo una lista cerrada y siempre como ajustes de usuario (nunca de dispositivo).
    for (const s of filterRestoredSettings(data.tables!.settings)) {
      await db.runAsync('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO settings (key,value,scope,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?)', [s.key, s.value, s.scope, s.updated_at]);
      total++;
    }
    await db.execAsync(seedCategoriesSql(Date.now())); // por si el respaldo no traÃ­a categorÃ­as
  });
  return total;
}

export interface CsvPreview {
  header: string[];
  map: ColumnMap | null;
  rows: string[][];
}

export function previewCsv(text: string): CsvPreview {
  const all = parseCsv(text);
  if (all.length > MAX_CSV_ROWS + 1) throw new Error(`El archivo tiene mÃ¡s de ${MAX_CSV_ROWS} filas.`);
  const header = all[0] ?? [];
  return { header, map: guessColumns(header), rows: all.slice(1) };
}

/**
 * Importa un extracto. No toca saldos de cuentas (son movimientos histÃ³ricos) y no duplica
 * al reimportar el mismo archivo (hash por fecha+descripciÃ³n+monto).
 */
export async function importCsvRows(
  rows: string[][], map: ColumnMap, positiveIsIncome?: boolean,
): Promise<{ imported: number; duplicates: number; skipped: number }> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const mapped = mapRows(rows, map, positiveIsIncome);
  let imported = 0;
  let duplicates = 0;
  const now = Date.now();
  for (const r of mapped.rows) {
    const exists = await db.getFirstAsync('SELECT id FROM transactions WHERE dedupe_hash=?', [r.dedupe]);
    if (exists) { duplicates++; continue; }
    const cat = r.type === 'income' ? 'cat_ingreso_otros' : (await suggestCategory(r.description)) ?? 'cat_otros';
    await db.withTransactionAsync(async () => {
      const id = uid();
      const row = {
        id, account_id: null, category_id: cat, debt_id: null, type: r.type, amount: r.amount,
        merchant: r.description.slice(0, 80) || null, note: null, occurred_at: r.occurredAt, source: 'csv',
        payment_method: 'unknown', status: 'posted', dedupe_hash: r.dedupe, created_at: now, updated_at: now, deleted_at: null,
      };
      const cols = Object.keys(row);
      await db.runAsync(`INSERT INTO transactions (${cols.join(',')}) VALUES (${cols.map(() => '?').join(',')})`, Object.values(row) as (string | number | null)[]);
      await db.runAsync('INSERT INTO sync_queue (table_name,row_id,op,payload,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)', ['transactions', id, 'upsert', JSON.stringify(row), now]);
    });
    imported++;
  }
  return { imported, duplicates, skipped: mapped.skipped };
}

/** Lee un archivo elegido con el selector y BORRA la copia temporal. Tope de tamaÃ±o. */
export async function readPickedFile(uri: string, maxBytes = MAX_IMPORT_BYTES): Promise<string> {
  try {
    const f = new File(uri);
    if ((f.size ?? 0) > maxBytes) throw new Error(`El archivo es demasiado grande (mÃ¡ximo ${Math.round(maxBytes / 1_000_000)} MB).`);
    return await f.text();
  } finally {
    deleteQuietly(uri);
  }
}

/**
 * Borra TODO lo del usuario en este celular: datos, PIN, claves de IA y de sincronizaciÃ³n,
 * cola y lista de apps del lector de notificaciones, avisos programados y archivos temporales.
 * secure_delete + VACUUM sobrescriben las pÃ¡ginas borradas de la base. Irreversible.
 */
export async function wipeAllData(): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.withTransactionAsync(async () => {
    await db.execAsync('PRAGMA defer_foreign_keys = ON');
    for (const t of [...USER_TABLES].reverse()) await db.runAsync(`DELETE FROM ${t}`);
    for (const t of ['net_worth_snapshots', 'sync_queue', 'notifications_log', 'settings']) await db.runAsync(`DELETE FROM ${t}`);
    await db.runAsync('UPDATE notification_sources SET bound_package=NULL');
    await db.execAsync(seedCategoriesSql(Date.now())); // las categorÃ­as predefinidas siempre existen
  });
  await db.execAsync('VACUUM');

  await clearSyncCredentials();
  await clearPin();
  await clearGeminiKey();
  NotificationListener.drain();
  NotificationListener.clearSeen();
  NotificationListener.setLearningMode(false);
  await Notifications.cancelAllScheduledNotificationsAsync().catch(() => {});
  cleanTempFiles();
}

ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\src\services\db.ts

import * as SQLite from 'expo-sqlite';
import * as Crypto from 'expo-crypto';
import * as FS from 'expo-file-system/legacy';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import { runMigrations, seedCategoriesSql, type SqlExecutor } from '@finzen/db';
import { DEFAULT_SOURCES } from '@finzen/core';

/**
 * Base de datos local, CIFRADA EN REPOSO con SQLCipher (AES-256).
 *
 *  - La clave (256 bits aleatorios) se genera en el primer arranque y vive en SecureStore,
 *    que en Android la protege con el Keystore del hardware. Nunca sale del celular.
 *  - Quien copie el archivo de la base (backup, root, cable) solo ve bytes aleatorios.
 *  - Si existe una base antigua SIN cifrar (versiones previas), se migra sola y la vieja se borra.
 *  - Si por cualquier razÃ³n SQLCipher no estÃ¡ disponible (p. ej. un APK antiguo), la app NO se
 *    rompe: usa la base sin cifrar y lo avisa en Ajustes â†’ DiagnÃ³stico (`isDbEncrypted()`).
 *  - Cada instalaciÃ³n crea su propia base: el repositorio nunca contiene datos de nadie.
 */
const LEGACY_NAME = 'finzen.db';
const SECURE_NAME = 'finzen_secure.db';
const KEY_NAME = 'finzen_db_key';

let encrypted = false;
export const isDbEncrypted = () => encrypted;

let dbPromise: Promise<SQLite.SQLiteDatabase> | null = null;

export function getDb(): Promise<SQLite.SQLiteDatabase> {
  dbPromise ??= init().catch((e) => {
    dbPromise = null; // permite reintentar tras un fallo
    throw e;
  });
  return dbPromise;
}

async function getOrCreateKey(): Promise<string> {
  const existing = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(KEY_NAME);
  if (existing && /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(existing)) return existing;
  const bytes = Crypto.getRandomBytes(32);
  const hex = Array.from(bytes, (b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
  await SecureStore.setItemAsync(KEY_NAME, hex);
  return hex;
}

const keyPragma = (key: string) => `PRAGMA key = "x'${key}'";`;
const exists = async (name: string) => (await FS.getInfoAsync(`file://${SQLite.defaultDatabaseDirectory}/${name}`)) as { exists: boolean; size?: number };

/** Abre la base cifrada y comprueba que SQLCipher estÃ© activo y que la clave sirva. */
async function openSecure(key: string): Promise<SQLite.SQLiteDatabase> {
  const db = await SQLite.openDatabaseAsync(SECURE_NAME);
  try {
    await db.execAsync(keyPragma(key));
    // Con SQLite normal este PRAGMA no devuelve nada: significarÃ­a que "cifrar" no cifrÃ³.
    if (!(await db.getFirstAsync('PRAGMA cipher_version'))) throw new Error('SQLCipher no estÃ¡ disponible en este build');
    await db.getFirstAsync('SELECT count(*) FROM sqlite_master'); // falla si la clave no corresponde
    return db;
  } catch (e) {
    await db.closeAsync().catch(() => {});
    throw e;
  }
}

/** Copia la base antigua sin cifrar a la cifrada (sqlcipher_export) y borra la antigua. */
async function migrateLegacy(key: string): Promise<void> {
  if (!(await exists(LEGACY_NAME)).exists) return;

  // Â¿Ya se migrÃ³ antes y solo faltÃ³ borrar la vieja? Se comprueba que la cifrada tenga datos.
  const secureInfo = await exists(SECURE_NAME);
  if (secureInfo.exists && (secureInfo.size ?? 0) > 0) {
    try {
      const probe = await openSecure(key);
      const tables = (await probe.getFirstAsync<{ n: number }>("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"))?.n ?? 0;
      await probe.closeAsync();
      if (tables > 0) { await removeLegacy(); return; }
    } catch { /* cifrada ilegible o vacÃ­a: se rehace */ }
    await SQLite.deleteDatabaseAsync(SECURE_NAME).catch(() => {});
  }

  const legacy = await SQLite.openDatabaseAsync(LEGACY_NAME);
  try {
    const ver = (await legacy.getFirstAsync<{ user_version: number }>('PRAGMA user_version'))?.user_version ?? 0;
    const target = `${SQLite.defaultDatabaseDirectory}/${SECURE_NAME}`.replace(/'/g, "''");
    await legacy.execAsync(`ATTACH DATABASE '${target}' AS enc KEY "x'${key}'";`);
    await legacy.execAsync("SELECT sqlcipher_export('enc');");
    await legacy.execAsync(`PRAGMA enc.user_version = ${Number(ver)};`);
    await legacy.execAsync('DETACH DATABASE enc;');
  } finally {
    await legacy.closeAsync().catch(() => {});
  }

  // Solo se borra la antigua si la nueva abre bien y trae las mismas tablas.
  const check = await openSecure(key);
  const n = (await check.getFirstAsync<{ n: number }>("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"))?.n ?? 0;
  await check.closeAsync();
  if (n === 0) throw new Error('La migraciÃ³n a base cifrada no copiÃ³ tablas');
  await removeLegacy();
}

async function removeLegacy(): Promise<void> {
  await SQLite.deleteDatabaseAsync(LEGACY_NAME).catch(() => {});
  for (const suffix of ['', '-wal', '-shm', '-journal']) {
    await FS.deleteAsync(`file://${SQLite.defaultDatabaseDirectory}/${LEGACY_NAME}${suffix}`, { idempotent: true }).catch(() => {});
  }
}

async function open(): Promise<SQLite.SQLiteDatabase> {
  try {
    const key = await getOrCreateKey();
    await migrateLegacy(key);
    const db = await openSecure(key);
    encrypted = true;
    return db;
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('Base cifrada no disponible; se usa la base sin cifrar', e);
    encrypted = false;
    return SQLite.openDatabaseAsync(LEGACY_NAME);
  }
}

async function init() {
  const db = await open();
  await db.execAsync('PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL; PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON; PRAGMA secure_delete = ON;');

  // Adaptador entre expo-sqlite y el runner de migraciones compartido.
  const exec: SqlExecutor = {
    exec: (sql) => db.execAsync(sql),
    getUserVersion: async () =>
      (await db.getFirstAsync<{ user_version: number }>('PRAGMA user_version'))?.user_version ?? 0,
    setUserVersion: (v) => db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = ${Number(v)}`),
  };
  await runMigrations(exec);

  await db.execAsync(seedCategoriesSql(Date.now()));
  for (const s of DEFAULT_SOURCES) {
    await db.runAsync(
      'INSERT OR IGNORE INTO notification_sources (id,label,kind,package_pattern,enabled,user_added) VALUES (?,?,?,?,1,0)',
      [s.id, s.label, s.kind, s.packagePattern],
    );
  }
  return db;
}

import { monthlyRateFromEA, planFromMode, summarizeCard, todayString, type CardSummary, type PlanInput } from '@finzen/core';
import { getDb } from './db';
import { adjustAccount, patchRow, softDelete, uid, writeRow, type Db } from './repo';

export type DebtKind = 'credit_card' | 'loan' | 'bnpl' | 'informal';
export const DEBT_KIND_LABEL: Record<DebtKind, string> = {
  credit_card: 'Tarjeta de crÃ©dito', loan: 'CrÃ©dito', bnpl: 'Compra a cuotas (Addi, SistecrÃ©ditoâ€¦)', informal: 'Deuda informal',
};

export interface Debt {
  id: string; name: string; kind: DebtKind; provider: string | null;
  principal: number; balance: number;
  installments_total: number | null; monthly_payment: number | null; monthly_rate: number;
  fixed_fee_per_installment: number;
  due_day: number | null; cut_day: number | null; credit_limit: number | null;
  first_due_date: string | null; status: 'active' | 'paid'; parent_debt_id: string | null;
}

export interface Installment {
  id: string; debt_id: string; number: number; due_date: string;
  principal: number; interest: number; fee: number; total: number; paid_at: number | null;
}

// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Alta de deudas / tarjetas â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export interface DebtInput {
  name: string; kind: DebtKind; provider?: string | null;
  balance: number; monthlyPayment: number;
  /** Tasa efectiva anual en %. 0 si no tiene. */
  annualRatePct: number;
  dueDay: number | null;
  cutDay?: number | null;
  creditLimit?: number | null;
}

export async function createDebt(d: DebtInput): Promise<string> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const now = Date.now();
  const id = uid();
  await db.withTransactionAsync(() =>
    writeRow(db, 'debts', {
      id, name: d.name, kind: d.kind, provider: d.provider ?? null,
      principal: d.balance, balance: d.balance, installments_total: null,
      monthly_payment: d.monthlyPayment || null,
      monthly_rate: d.annualRatePct > 0 ? monthlyRateFromEA(d.annualRatePct / 100) : 0,
      fixed_fee_per_installment: 0, due_day: d.dueDay, first_due_date: null,
      status: 'active', origin_transaction_id: null,
      credit_limit: d.creditLimit ?? null, cut_day: d.cutDay ?? null, parent_debt_id: null,
      created_at: now, updated_at: now, deleted_at: null,
    }),
  );
  return id;
}

// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Consultas â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const COLS =
  'id,name,kind,provider,principal,balance,installments_total,monthly_payment,monthly_rate,fixed_fee_per_installment,due_day,cut_day,credit_limit,first_due_date,status,parent_debt_id';

/** Deudas de primer nivel (tarjetas, crÃ©ditos, BNPL). Los planes de una tarjeta cuelgan de ella. */
export async function listDebts(): Promise<Debt[]> {
  const db = await getDb();
  return db.getAllAsync<Debt>(
    `SELECT ${COLS} FROM debts WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND status='active' AND parent_debt_id IS NULL ORDER BY created_at`,
  );
}

export async function listPlansOf(cardId: string): Promise<Debt[]> {
  const db = await getDb();
  return db.getAllAsync<Debt>(
    `SELECT ${COLS} FROM debts WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND parent_debt_id=? ORDER BY created_at`, [cardId],
  );
}

export async function getDebt(id: string): Promise<Debt | null> {
  const db = await getDb();
  return db.getFirstAsync<Debt>(`SELECT ${COLS} FROM debts WHERE id=? AND deleted_at IS NULL`, [id]);
}

export async function listCards(): Promise<Debt[]> {
  const db = await getDb();
  return db.getAllAsync<Debt>(
    `SELECT ${COLS} FROM debts WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND kind='credit_card' AND status='active' ORDER BY created_at`,
  );
}

export async function getInstallments(debtId: string): Promise<Installment[]> {
  const db = await getDb();
  return db.getAllAsync<Installment>(
    'SELECT id,debt_id,number,due_date,principal,interest,fee,total,paid_at FROM debt_installments WHERE debt_id=? AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY number',
    [debtId],
  );
}

export async function cardSummary(card: Debt): Promise<CardSummary> {
  const plans = await listPlansOf(card.id);
  return summarizeCard({
    creditLimit: card.credit_limit ?? 0,
    revolvingBalance: card.balance,
    planBalances: plans.filter((p) => p.status === 'active').map((p) => p.balance),
    cutDay: card.cut_day, dueDay: card.due_day, monthlyRate: card.monthly_rate, today: todayString(),
  });
}

export interface UpcomingPayment { debtName: string; installmentId: string; dueDate: string; total: number; number: number; of: number | null }

/** PrÃ³ximas cuotas sin pagar (de todos los planes), ordenadas por fecha. */
export async function upcomingPayments(limit = 5): Promise<UpcomingPayment[]> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const rows = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string; id: string; due_date: string; total: number; number: number; installments_total: number | null }>(
    `SELECT d.name, i.id, i.due_date, i.total, i.number, d.installments_total
       FROM debt_installments i JOIN debts d ON d.id = i.debt_id
      WHERE i.paid_at IS NULL AND i.deleted_at IS NULL AND d.deleted_at IS NULL AND d.status='active'
      ORDER BY i.due_date LIMIT ?`, [limit],
  );
  return rows.map((r) => ({ debtName: r.name, installmentId: r.id, dueDate: r.due_date, total: r.total, number: r.number, of: r.installments_total }));
}

// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Planes de cuotas â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export interface PlanParams extends Omit<PlanInput, 'principal'> {
  name: string;
  provider: string | null;
  /** Tarjeta a la que pertenece el plan; null = BNPL suelto (Addi, SistecrÃ©dito). */
  parentDebtId: string | null;
  principal: number;
}

/**
 * Crea la deuda-plan y su calendario de cuotas. Debe llamarse dentro de una transacciÃ³n abierta.
 * Devuelve el id del plan y el resumen para mostrar al usuario.
 */
export async function insertPlan(db: Db, p: PlanParams, originTxId: string | null): Promise<{ id: string; monthlyPayment: number }> {
  const plan = planFromMode({ principal: p.principal, installments: p.installments, firstDueDate: p.firstDueDate, mode: p.mode });
  const now = Date.now();
  const id = uid();
  const first = plan.schedule[0]!;
  await writeRow(db, 'debts', {
    id, name: p.name, kind: p.parentDebtId ? 'loan' : 'bnpl', provider: p.provider,
    principal: p.principal, balance: p.principal,
    installments_total: p.installments, monthly_payment: first.total, monthly_rate: plan.monthlyRate,
    fixed_fee_per_installment: plan.feePerInstallment,
    due_day: Number(p.firstDueDate.slice(8, 10)), first_due_date: p.firstDueDate,
    status: 'active', origin_transaction_id: originTxId,
    credit_limit: null, cut_day: null, parent_debt_id: p.parentDebtId,
    created_at: now, updated_at: now, deleted_at: null,
  });
  for (const r of plan.schedule) {
    await writeRow(db, 'debt_installments', {
      id: uid(), debt_id: id, number: r.number, due_date: r.dueDate,
      principal: r.principal, interest: r.interest, fee: r.fee, total: r.total,
      paid_at: null, created_at: now, updated_at: now, deleted_at: null,
    });
  }
  return { id, monthlyPayment: first.total };
}

// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Pagos â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
/** Marca una cuota como pagada, baja el saldo del plan y (opcional) descuenta de una cuenta. */
export async function payInstallment(installmentId: string, accountId: string | null): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.withTransactionAsync(async () => {
    const inst = await db.getFirstAsync<Installment>(
      'SELECT id,debt_id,number,due_date,principal,interest,fee,total,paid_at FROM debt_installments WHERE id=?', [installmentId],
    );
    if (!inst || inst.paid_at) return;
    const debt = await db.getFirstAsync<{ balance: number; name: string }>('SELECT balance,name FROM debts WHERE id=?', [inst.debt_id]);
    if (!debt) return;

    await patchRow(db, 'debt_installments', installmentId, { paid_at: Date.now() });
    const balance = Math.max(0, debt.balance - inst.principal);
    const pending = await db.getFirstAsync<{ n: number }>(
      'SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM debt_installments WHERE debt_id=? AND paid_at IS NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL AND id<>?',
      [inst.debt_id, installmentId],
    );
    await patchRow(db, 'debts', inst.debt_id, { balance, status: (pending?.n ?? 0) === 0 ? 'paid' : 'active' });
    await recordDebtPayment(db, `Cuota ${inst.number} Â· ${debt.name}`, inst.total, accountId, inst.debt_id);
  });
}

/** Abono a la tarjeta (reduce el saldo rotativo). */
export async function payCard(cardId: string, amount: number, accountId: string | null): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.withTransactionAsync(async () => {
    const card = await db.getFirstAsync<{ balance: number; name: string }>('SELECT balance,name FROM debts WHERE id=?', [cardId]);
    if (!card || amount <= 0) return;
    await patchRow(db, 'debts', cardId, { balance: Math.max(0, card.balance - amount) });
    await recordDebtPayment(db, `Pago tarjeta Â· ${card.name}`, amount, accountId, cardId);
  });
}

/**
 * Registra el pago como gasto en la categorÃ­a "Pago de deudas" (y baja la cuenta si se indicÃ³).
 * Esa categorÃ­a se excluye de "gastos del mes": la compra ya se contÃ³ cuando se hizo.
 */
async function recordDebtPayment(db: Db, label: string, amount: number, accountId: string | null, debtId: string) {
  const now = Date.now();
  await writeRow(db, 'transactions', {
    id: uid(), account_id: accountId, category_id: 'cat_deudas', debt_id: debtId, type: 'expense', amount,
    merchant: label, note: null, occurred_at: now, source: 'manual', payment_method: accountId ? 'debit' : 'unknown',
    status: 'posted', dedupe_hash: null, created_at: now, updated_at: now, deleted_at: null,
  });
  if (accountId) await adjustAccount(db, accountId, -amount);
}

export async function deleteDebt(id: string): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.withTransactionAsync(async () => {
    const children = await db.getAllAsync<{ id: string }>('SELECT id FROM debts WHERE parent_debt_id=? AND deleted_at IS NULL', [id]);
    for (const debtId of [id, ...children.map((c) => c.id)]) {
      const insts = await db.getAllAsync<{ id: string }>('SELECT id FROM debt_installments WHERE debt_id=? AND deleted_at IS NULL', [debtId]);
      for (const i of insts) await softDelete(db, 'debt_installments', i.id);
      await softDelete(db, 'debts', debtId);
    }
  });


}

/**
 * NUEVA FUNCIÃ“N: Abona un monto libre a capital de cualquier deuda (prÃ©stamo, BNPL, informal).
 * Reduce el saldo, actualiza el estado a 'paid' si llega a 0, y registra el gasto.
 */
export async function payCapital(debtId: string, amount: number, accountId: string | null): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.withTransactionAsync(async () => {
    const debt = await db.getFirstAsync<{ balance: number; name: string }>('SELECT balance,name FROM debts WHERE id=?', [debtId]);
    if (!debt || amount <= 0) return;

    // 1. Calculamos el nuevo saldo
    const newBalance = Math.max(0, debt.balance - amount);
    // 2. Si el saldo llega a 0, marcamos la deuda como pagada
    const newStatus = newBalance === 0 ? 'paid' : 'active';

    // 3. Actualizamos la deuda
    await patchRow(db, 'debts', debtId, { balance: newBalance, status: newStatus });
    
    // 4. Registramos el movimiento en el historial y descontamos de la cuenta
    await recordDebtPayment(db, `Abono a capital Â· ${debt.name}`, amount, accountId, debtId);
  });
}

import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { DEFAULT_SOURCES } from '@finzen/core';
import { runMigrations, seedCategoriesSql } from '@finzen/db';

/**
 * Pruebas de integraciÃ³n de los flujos de dinero, ejecutando los servicios REALES de la app
 * contra SQLite real (node:sqlite) en vez de expo-sqlite. Solo se sustituyen getDb y expo-crypto.
 */
const holder = vi.hoisted(() => ({ db: null as any }));
vi.mock('./db', () => ({ getDb: async () => holder.db }));
let counter = 0;
vi.mock('expo-crypto', () => ({ randomUUID: () => `id-${++counter}` }));

const { DatabaseSync } = (process as any).getBuiltinModule('node:sqlite');

async function freshDb() {
  const raw = new DatabaseSync(':memory:');
  raw.exec('PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON');
  const adapter = {
    runAsync: async (sql: string, p: any[] = []) => { raw.prepare(sql).run(...p); },
    getAllAsync: async (sql: string, p: any[] = []) => raw.prepare(sql).all(...p),
    getFirstAsync: async (sql: string, p: any[] = []) => raw.prepare(sql).get(...p) ?? null,
    execAsync: async (sql: string) => { raw.exec(sql); },
    withTransactionAsync: async (fn: () => Promise<unknown>) => {
      raw.exec('BEGIN');
      try { await fn(); raw.exec('COMMIT'); } catch (e) { raw.exec('ROLLBACK'); throw e; }
    },
  };
  await runMigrations({
    exec: async (s) => raw.exec(s),
    getUserVersion: async () => raw.prepare('PRAGMA user_version').get().user_version,
    setUserVersion: async (v) => raw.exec(`PRAGMA user_version = ${v}`),
  });
  raw.exec(seedCategoriesSql(1));
  for (const s of DEFAULT_SOURCES) {
    raw.prepare('INSERT INTO notification_sources (id,label,kind,package_pattern,enabled,user_added) VALUES (?,?,?,?,1,0)')
      .run(s.id, s.label, s.kind, s.packagePattern);
  }
  return adapter;
}

import { cardSummary, createDebt, deleteDebt, getDebt, getInstallments, listPlansOf, payCard, payInstallment } from './debts';

const FIRST_DUE = '2026-11-15';

async function setup() {
  await addAccount({ name: 'Bancolombia ahorros', kind: 'savings', balance: 1_000_000, currency: 'COP' });
  const accountId = (await listAccounts())[0]!.id;
  const cardId = await createDebt({
    name: 'Nu', kind: 'credit_card', balance: 0, monthlyPayment: 0, annualRatePct: 28, dueDay: 25, cutDay: 10, creditLimit: 2_000_000,
  });
  return { accountId, cardId };
}

beforeEach(async () => { holder.db = await freshDb(); });

describe('tarjeta de crÃ©dito', () => {
  it('compra a 1 pago suma al saldo de la tarjeta y reduce el cupo', async () => {
    const { cardId } = await setup();
    await createTransaction({ type: 'expense', amount: 150_000, merchant: 'Rappi', categoryId: 'cat_comida_domicilio', paymentMethod: 'credit', cardId });
    const card = (await getDebt(cardId))!;
    expect(card.balance).toBe(150_000);
    const s = await cardSummary(card);
    expect(s.used).toBe(150_000);
    expect(s.available).toBe(1_850_000);
  });

  it('compra a cuotas crea un plan hijo; el saldo rotativo no cambia pero el cupo sÃ­', async () => {
    const { cardId } = await setup();
    const r = await createInstallmentPurchase(
      { type: 'expense', amount: 600_000, merchant: 'Alkosto', categoryId: 'cat_compras', paymentMethod: 'credit' },
      { name: 'Alkosto', provider: 'Nu', parentDebtId: cardId, installments: 6, mode: { kind: 'ea', eaPct: 28 }, firstDueDate: FIRST_DUE },
    );
    const plans = await listPlansOf(cardId);
    expect(plans).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(plans[0]!.id).toBe(r.planId);
    expect(plans[0]!.parent_debt_id).toBe(cardId);
    expect((await getInstallments(r.planId))).toHaveLength(6);

    const card = (await getDebt(cardId))!;
    expect(card.balance).toBe(0);
    expect((await cardSummary(card)).used).toBe(600_000);
    // El patrimonio cuenta el plan una sola vez (no se duplica con la tarjeta)
    expect((await getNetWorth()).liabilities).toBe(600_000);
  });

  it('pagar una cuota baja el saldo del plan, descuenta de la cuenta y registra el pago', async () => {
    const { cardId, accountId } = await setup();
    const r = await createInstallmentPurchase(
      { type: 'expense', amount: 300_000, merchant: 'Falabella', categoryId: 'cat_compras', paymentMethod: 'credit' },
      { name: 'Falabella', provider: null, parentDebtId: cardId, installments: 3, mode: { kind: 'zero', feePerInstallment: 0 }, firstDueDate: FIRST_DUE },
    );
    const insts = await getInstallments(r.planId);
    await payInstallment(insts[0]!.id, accountId);

    expect((await getDebt(r.planId))!.balance).toBe(300_000 - insts[0]!.principal);
    expect((await listAccounts())[0]!.balance).toBe(1_000_000 - insts[0]!.total);

    // Pagar de nuevo la misma cuota no hace nada (idempotente)
    await payInstallment(insts[0]!.id, accountId);
    expect((await listAccounts())[0]!.balance).toBe(1_000_000 - insts[0]!.total);

    await payInstallment(insts[1]!.id, accountId);
    await payInstallment(insts[2]!.id, accountId);
    const plan = (await getDebt(r.planId))!;
    expect(plan.balance).toBe(0);
    expect(plan.status).toBe('paid');
  });

  it('pagar la tarjeta reduce el saldo rotativo y no baja de 0', async () => {
    const { cardId, accountId } = await setup();
    await createTransaction({ type: 'expense', amount: 100_000, merchant: 'X', categoryId: 'cat_otros', paymentMethod: 'credit', cardId });
    await payCard(cardId, 250_000, accountId);
    expect((await getDebt(cardId))!.balance).toBe(0);
  });

  it('eliminar una compra a 1 pago revierte el saldo; una de cuotas no se deja eliminar suelta', async () => {
    const { cardId } = await setup();
    const single = await createTransaction({ type: 'expense', amount: 80_000, merchant: 'X', categoryId: 'cat_otros', paymentMethod: 'credit', cardId });
    expect(await deleteTransaction(single)).toBe(true);
    expect((await getDebt(cardId))!.balance).toBe(0);

    const plan = await createInstallmentPurchase(
      { type: 'expense', amount: 90_000, merchant: 'Y', categoryId: 'cat_otros', paymentMethod: 'credit' },
      { name: 'Y', provider: null, parentDebtId: cardId, installments: 3, mode: { kind: 'zero', feePerInstallment: 0 }, firstDueDate: FIRST_DUE },
    );
    expect(await deleteTransaction(plan.txId)).toBe(false);
    await deleteDebt(cardId); // borra la tarjeta y sus planes
    expect(await listPlansOf(cardId)).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

import { AppState } from 'react-native';
import { NotificationListener } from '../../modules/finzen-notifications';
import { getDb } from './db';
import { ingestNotification } from './notifications';
import { notifyNow } from './reminders';
import { listPending } from './transactions';

/**
 * Puente entre el servicio nativo (Kotlin) y el resto de la app.
 *  - Le entrega al servicio la lista blanca (patrones de apps habilitadas).
 *  - VacÃ­a la cola de notificaciones capturadas y las pasa por el pipeline de seguridad.
 * Se ejecuta al abrir la app, al volver a primer plano y cuando llega una notificaciÃ³n nueva.
 */

/** Sincroniza la lista blanca de la base de datos hacia el servicio nativo. */
export async function syncWhitelist(): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const rows = await db.getAllAsync<{ package_pattern: string }>(
    'SELECT package_pattern FROM notification_sources WHERE enabled=1',
  );
  NotificationListener.setWhitelist(rows.map((r) => r.package_pattern));
}

let draining: Promise<number> | null = null;

/** Procesa la cola nativa. Devuelve cuÃ¡ntas transacciones nuevas se importaron. */
export function drainAndIngest(): Promise<number> {
  draining ??= (async () => {
    try {
      const items = NotificationListener.drain();
      if (items.length === 0) return 0;
      const pendingBefore = (await listPending()).length;
      let imported = 0;
      for (const n of items) {
        try {
          if ((await ingestNotification(n)) === 'imported') imported++;
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn('No se pudo procesar una notificaciÃ³n', e);
        }
      }
      const pendingAfter = (await listPending()).length;
      if (pendingAfter > pendingBefore) {
        await notifyNow('Compra a crÃ©dito detectada', 'Abre FinZen para indicar en cuÃ¡ntas cuotas fue.');
      }
      return imported;
    } finally {
      draining = null;
    }
  })();
  return draining;
}

/** Arranca el puente. Devuelve la funciÃ³n para detenerlo. */
export function startListenerBridge(onImported?: () => void): () => void {
  const run = () => {
    drainAndIngest().then((n) => { if (n > 0) onImported?.(); }).catch(() => {});
  };
  syncWhitelist().then(run).catch(() => {});
  const sub = NotificationListener.onQueued(run);
  const app = AppState.addEventListener('change', (s) => {
    if (s === 'active') { syncWhitelist().then(run).catch(() => {}); }
  });
  return () => { sub.remove(); app.remove(); };
}

ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\src\services\notifications.ts
==========================================
import { processNotification, type NotificationSource, type RawNotification } from '@finzen/core';
import { getDb } from './db';
import { adjustAccount, uid, writeRow } from './repo';
import { suggestCategory } from './transactions';

/**
 * Recibe una notificaciÃ³n cruda (de Android o del simulador), la pasa por el pipeline de
 * seguridad de @finzen/core y guarda el resultado. El texto original NO se guarda nunca:
 * solo la versiÃ³n enmascarada, y nada si fue OTP o de una app fuera de la lista blanca.
 */
export type IngestOutcome = 'imported' | 'duplicate' | 'discarded_otp' | 'discarded_not_whitelisted' | 'discarded_unparsed';

async function loadSources(): Promise<NotificationSource[]> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const rows = await db.getAllAsync<{ id: string; label: string; kind: 'bank' | 'wallet' | 'bnpl'; package_pattern: string }>(
    'SELECT id,label,kind,package_pattern FROM notification_sources WHERE enabled=1',
  );
  return rows.map((r) => ({ id: r.id, label: r.label, kind: r.kind, packagePattern: r.package_pattern }));
}

const LOG_DAYS = 30;
const LOG_MAX_ROWS = 500;

/** MinimizaciÃ³n de datos: la bitÃ¡cora de notificaciones no crece sin lÃ­mite. */
async function pruneLog(db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getDb>>): Promise<void> {
  await db.runAsync('DELETE FROM notifications_log WHERE received_at < ?', [Date.now() - LOG_DAYS * 86_400_000]);
  await db.runAsync(
    'DELETE FROM notifications_log WHERE id NOT IN (SELECT id FROM notifications_log ORDER BY received_at DESC LIMIT ?)',
    [LOG_MAX_ROWS],
  );
}

/**
 * Anclaje de fuente (confianza en el primer uso). Los patrones de la lista blanca coinciden por
 * subcadena: una app maliciosa "com.evil.nequi" pasarÃ­a el filtro. Por eso, la primera notificaciÃ³n
 * VÃLIDA de cada fuente fija su paquete exacto y desde entonces solo ese paquete cuenta.
 * Devuelve false si el paquete NO es el anclado (posible suplantaciÃ³n).
 */
async function checkBinding(db: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getDb>>, sourceId: string, pkg: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ bound_package: string | null }>('SELECT bound_package FROM notification_sources WHERE id=?', [sourceId]);
  if (!row) return false;
  if (row.bound_package === null) {
    await db.runAsync('UPDATE notification_sources SET bound_package=? WHERE id=? AND bound_package IS NULL', [pkg, sourceId]);
    return true;
  }
  return row.bound_package === pkg;
}

export async function ingestNotification(raw: RawNotification): Promise<IngestOutcome> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const sources = await loadSources();
  const result = processNotification(raw, sources);
  await pruneLog(db);
  const logId = uid();
  const log = (outcome: IngestOutcome, maskedText: string | null, hash: string | null, txId: string | null) =>
    db.runAsync(
      'INSERT INTO notifications_log (id,source_package,received_at,masked_text,text_hash,outcome,transaction_id) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
      [logId, raw.packageName, raw.receivedAt, maskedText, hash, outcome, txId],
    );

  if (result.outcome === 'discarded_not_whitelisted') {
    // Ni siquiera se registra el paquete: no queremos un historial de quÃ© apps usa la persona.
    return 'discarded_not_whitelisted';
  }
  if (result.outcome === 'discarded_otp') { await log('discarded_otp', null, null, null); return 'discarded_otp'; }
  if (result.outcome === 'discarded_unparsed') { await log('discarded_unparsed', result.maskedText, null, null); return 'discarded_unparsed'; }

  // Â¿El paquete es el verdadero de esa fuente? Si no, se ignora y se deja constancia (sin texto).
  if (!(await checkBinding(db, result.sourceId, raw.packageName))) {
    await log('discarded_not_whitelisted', null, null, null);
    return 'discarded_not_whitelisted';
  }

  const dup = await db.getFirstAsync<{ id: string }>('SELECT id FROM transactions WHERE dedupe_hash=?', [result.dedupeHash]);
  if (dup) { await log('duplicate', result.maskedText, result.dedupeHash, dup.id); return 'duplicate'; }

  const p = result.parsed;
  const source = sources.find((s) => s.id === result.sourceId);
  const now = Date.now();
  const txId = uid();
  const categoryId = p.isDebtPayment ? 'cat_deudas' : p.type === 'income' ? 'cat_ingreso_otros' : p.merchant ? await suggestCategory(p.merchant) : null;
  const status = p.needsInstallments ? 'needs_installments' : 'posted';

  // Cuenta asociada: la que se llame como la app (p. ej. app "Nequi" â†’ cuenta "Nequi").
  let accountId: string | null = null;
  if (!p.needsInstallments && p.paymentMethod !== 'credit' && source) {
    const acc = await db.getFirstAsync<{ id: string }>(
      "SELECT id FROM accounts WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND archived=0 AND lower(name) LIKE '%' || lower(?) || '%' LIMIT 1",
      [source.label],
    );
    accountId = acc?.id ?? null;
  }

  await db.withTransactionAsync(async () => {
    await writeRow(db, 'transactions', {
      id: txId, account_id: accountId, category_id: categoryId, debt_id: null,
      type: p.type, amount: p.amount, merchant: p.merchant ?? (p.isDebtPayment ? 'Pago de deuda' : null),
      note: p.suggestedInstallments ? `Sugerido: ${p.suggestedInstallments} cuotas` : null,
      occurred_at: raw.receivedAt, source: 'notification',
      payment_method: p.paymentMethod, status, dedupe_hash: result.dedupeHash,
      created_at: now, updated_at: now, deleted_at: null,
    });
    if (status === 'posted' && accountId) await adjustAccount(db, accountId, p.type === 'expense' ? -p.amount : p.amount);
    await log('imported', result.maskedText, result.dedupeHash, txId);
  });
  return 'imported';
}

  return db.getAllAsync<RecurringExpense>(
    'SELECT id, name, amount, day_of_month, category_id FROM recurring_expenses WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND is_active=1 ORDER BY day_of_month'
  );

  const db = await getDb();

    id: uid(), 
    name, 
    amount, 
    day_of_month: day, 
    category_id: categoryId, 
    is_active: 1,
    created_at: now, 
    updated_at: now, 
    deleted_at: null
  }));
}

export async function deleteRecurring(id: string): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();

ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\src\services\reminders.ts

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import * as Notifications from 'expo-notifications';
import { nextOccurrence, todayString } from '@finzen/core';
import { getDb } from './db';

/**
 * Avisos LOCALES (los programa el propio telÃ©fono: gratis, sin servidor y sin internet).
 *  - Un dÃ­a antes de cada cuota sin pagar, a las 9:00.
 *  - Dos dÃ­as antes del pago lÃ­mite de cada tarjeta, a las 9:00.
 */
const CHANNEL = 'finzen-recordatorios';

Notifications.setNotificationHandler({
  handleNotification: async () => ({
    shouldShowBanner: true, shouldShowList: true, shouldPlaySound: false, shouldSetBadge: false,
  }),
});

async function ensureChannel() {
  if (Platform.OS !== 'android') return;
  await Notifications.setNotificationChannelAsync(CHANNEL, {
    name: 'Recordatorios de pagos', importance: Notifications.AndroidImportance.HIGH,
  });
}

export async function ensurePermission(): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    await ensureChannel();
    const cur = await Notifications.getPermissionsAsync();
    if (cur.granted) return true;
    return (await Notifications.requestPermissionsAsync()).granted;
  } catch { return false; }
}

/** Aviso inmediato (p. ej. "compra a crÃ©dito detectada"). */
export async function notifyNow(title: string, body: string): Promise<void> {
  try {
    if (!(await ensurePermission())) return;
    await Notifications.scheduleNotificationAsync({
      content: { title, body },
      trigger: Platform.OS === 'android' ? { channelId: CHANNEL } : null,
    });
  } catch { /* sin permiso o sin mÃ³dulo: no pasa nada */ }
}

const cop = (n: number) => '$' + Math.round(n).toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, '.');

function at9(dateStr: string, daysBefore: number): Date {
  const [y, m, d] = dateStr.split('-').map(Number) as [number, number, number];
  return new Date(y, m - 1, d - daysBefore, 9, 0, 0);
}

/** Reprograma todos los recordatorios a partir del estado actual de la base de datos. */
export async function rescheduleReminders(): Promise<number> {
  try {
    if (!(await ensurePermission())) return 0;
    await Notifications.cancelAllScheduledNotificationsAsync();
    const db = await getDb();
    const now = Date.now();
    let count = 0;
    const schedule = async (when: Date, title: string, body: string) => {
      if (when.getTime() <= now || count >= 60) return; // Android limita las alarmas programadas
      await Notifications.scheduleNotificationAsync({
        content: { title, body },
        trigger: { type: Notifications.SchedulableTriggerInputTypes.DATE, date: when, channelId: CHANNEL },
      });
      count++;
    };

    const insts = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string; due_date: string; total: number; number: number; n: number | null }>(
      `SELECT d.name, i.due_date, i.total, i.number, d.installments_total AS n
         FROM debt_installments i JOIN debts d ON d.id=i.debt_id
        WHERE i.paid_at IS NULL AND i.deleted_at IS NULL AND d.deleted_at IS NULL AND d.status='active'
        ORDER BY i.due_date LIMIT 40`,
    );
    for (const i of insts) {
      await schedule(at9(i.due_date, 1), 'Cuota maÃ±ana', `${i.name}${i.n ? ` (${i.number}/${i.n})` : ''}: ${cop(i.total)}`);
    }

    const cards = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string; due_day: number | null; balance: number }>(
      "SELECT name, due_day, balance FROM debts WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND kind='credit_card' AND status='active' AND due_day IS NOT NULL",
    );
    for (const c of cards) {
      const due = nextOccurrence(todayString(), c.due_day!);
      await schedule(at9(due, 2), 'Pago de tarjeta en 2 dÃ­as', `${c.name}: vence el ${due}`);
    }
    return count;
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('rescheduleReminders', e);
    return 0;
  }
}

ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\src\services\repo.ts

import type { SQLiteDatabase } from 'expo-sqlite';
import { randomUUID } from 'expo-crypto';
import { monthKey } from '@finzen/core';
import { getDb } from './db';

/**
 * Capa de acceso a datos (primitivas). Toda escritura de una tabla sincronizable pasa por
 * `writeRow`, que ademÃ¡s deja el cambio en `sync_queue` (paso de sincronizaciÃ³n: se sube a
 * Supabase al haber conexiÃ³n). Las operaciones compuestas llaman a `writeRow` dentro de UNA
 * transacciÃ³n (`db.withTransactionAsync`) para que sean atÃ³micas.
 */

export type Row = Record<string, string | number | null>;
export type Db = SQLiteDatabase;

export const uid = () => randomUUID();

/** Inserta o reemplaza una fila completa y la encola para sync. NO abre transacciÃ³n. */
export async function writeRow(db: Db, table: string, row: Row): Promise<void> {
  const cols = Object.keys(row); // claves internas, nunca entrada del usuario
  await db.runAsync(
    `INSERT OR REPLACE INTO ${table} (${cols.join(',')}) VALUES (${cols.map(() => '?').join(',')})`,
    cols.map((c) => row[c] as string | number | null),
  );
  await db.runAsync(
    'INSERT INTO sync_queue (table_name,row_id,op,payload,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)',
    [table, String(row.id), 'upsert', JSON.stringify(row), Date.now()],
  );
}

/** Lee la fila, aplica los cambios, sube updated_at y la reescribe. */
export async function patchRow(db: Db, table: string, id: string, patch: Partial<Row>): Promise<void> {
  const current = await db.getFirstAsync<Row>(`SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE id=?`, [id]);
  if (!current) throw new Error(`No existe ${table}/${id}`);
  await writeRow(db, table, { ...current, ...patch, updated_at: Date.now() });
}

/** Borrado lÃ³gico (asÃ­ el borrado tambiÃ©n se sincroniza). */
export const softDelete = (db: Db, table: string, id: string) =>
  patchRow(db, table, id, { deleted_at: Date.now() });

// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Ajustes â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export async function getSetting(key: string): Promise<string | null> {
  const db = await getDb();
  return (await db.getFirstAsync<{ value: string }>('SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key=?', [key]))?.value ?? null;
}

export async function setSetting(key: string, value: string, scope: 'user' | 'device' = 'user'): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.runAsync(
    'INSERT OR REPLACE INTO settings (key,value,scope,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?)',
    [key, value, scope, Date.now()],
  );
}

// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Cuentas (activos) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export type AccountKind = 'savings' | 'checking' | 'cash' | 'wallet' | 'investment';
export const ACCOUNT_KIND_LABEL: Record<AccountKind, string> = {
  savings: 'Ahorros', checking: 'Corriente', cash: 'Efectivo', wallet: 'Billetera digital', investment: 'InversiÃ³n',
};

export interface AccountInput { name: string; kind: AccountKind; balance: number; currency: string }
export interface Account extends AccountInput { id: string }

export async function addAccount(a: AccountInput): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const now = Date.now();
  await db.withTransactionAsync(() =>
    writeRow(db, 'accounts', {
      id: uid(), name: a.name, kind: a.kind, balance: a.balance, currency: a.currency,
      last4: null, archived: 0, created_at: now, updated_at: now, deleted_at: null,
    }),
  );
}

export async function listAccounts(): Promise<Account[]> {
  const db = await getDb();
  return db.getAllAsync<Account>(
    'SELECT id,name,kind,balance,currency FROM accounts WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND archived=0 ORDER BY created_at',
  );
}

/** Suma `delta` (puede ser negativo) al saldo de una cuenta. Dentro de una transacciÃ³n abierta. */
export async function adjustAccount(db: Db, accountId: string, delta: number): Promise<void> {
  const acc = await db.getFirstAsync<{ balance: number }>('SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id=?', [accountId]);
  if (!acc) return;
  await patchRow(db, 'accounts', accountId, { balance: acc.balance + delta });
}

// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Patrimonio â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export interface NetWorth { assets: number; liabilities: number; net: number }

export async function getNetWorth(): Promise<NetWorth> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const a = await db.getFirstAsync<{ s: number | null }>(
    'SELECT SUM(balance) AS s FROM accounts WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND archived=0',
  );
  const l = await db.getFirstAsync<{ s: number | null }>(
    "SELECT SUM(balance) AS s FROM debts WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND status='active'",
  );
  const assets = a?.s ?? 0;
  const liabilities = l?.s ?? 0;
  return { assets, liabilities, net: assets - liabilities };
}

/** Guarda/actualiza la foto del patrimonio del mes actual (alimenta la grÃ¡fica de evoluciÃ³n). */
export async function recordSnapshot(): Promise<NetWorth> {
  const nw = await getNetWorth();
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.runAsync(
    'INSERT OR REPLACE INTO net_worth_snapshots (month,assets,liabilities,net,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)',
    [monthKey(), nw.assets, nw.liabilities, nw.net, Date.now()],
  );
  return nw;
}

export interface Snapshot { month: string; net: number }

export async function listSnapshots(limit = 6): Promise<Snapshot[]> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const rows = await db.getAllAsync<Snapshot>(
    'SELECT month, net FROM net_worth_snapshots ORDER BY month DESC LIMIT ?', [limit],
  );
  return rows.reverse();
}
// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ NUEVAS FUNCIONES PARA FASE 1 â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

/**
 * Busca la primera cuenta activa del usuario. Si no tiene ninguna, crea automÃ¡ticamente
 * una cuenta "Efectivo / General" para que los movimientos no queden huÃ©rfanos.
 */
export async function getOrCreateDefaultAccount(db: Db): Promise<string> {
  // 1. Intentamos buscar cualquier cuenta que ya exista
  const existing = await db.getFirstAsync<{ id: string }>('SELECT id FROM accounts WHERE deleted_at IS NULL AND archived=0 LIMIT 1');
  if (existing) return existing.id;

  // 2. Si no hay ninguna, creamos la cuenta por defecto
  const newId = uid();
  const now = Date.now();
  await writeRow(db, 'accounts', {
    id: newId, 
    name: 'Efectivo / General', 
    kind: 'cash', 
    balance: 0, 
    currency: 'COP',
    last4: null, 
    archived: 0, 
    created_at: now, 
    updated_at: now, 
    deleted_at: null,
  });
  return newId;
}

/**
 * Ajusta el saldo de una cuenta a un valor exacto y deja un registro en el historial.
 * Sirve para cuando olvidaste registrar un ingreso/gasto y necesitas cuadrar las cuentas.
 */
export async function reconcileAccountBalance(db: Db, accountId: string, newBalance: number): Promise<void> {
  // 1. Leemos el saldo actual antes de cambiarlo
  const acc = await db.getFirstAsync<{ balance: number }>('SELECT balance FROM accounts WHERE id=?', [accountId]);
  if (!acc) return;
  
  // 2. Calculamos la diferencia (puede ser positiva o negativa)
  const delta = newBalance - acc.balance;
  if (delta === 0) return; // Si no hay diferencia, no hacemos nada

  // 3. Actualizamos el saldo real en la tabla de cuentas
  await patchRow(db, 'accounts', accountId, { balance: newBalance });

  // 4. Creamos una transacciÃ³n "fantasma" para que el historial refleje el ajuste
  const now = Date.now();
  await writeRow(db, 'transactions', {
    id: uid(),
    account_id: accountId,
    // Usamos 'cat_ingreso_otros' si el saldo subiÃ³ (delta > 0) o 'cat_otros' si bajÃ³
    category_id: delta > 0 ? 'cat_ingreso_otros' : 'cat_otros',
    debt_id: null,
    type: delta > 0 ? 'income' : 'expense',
    amount: Math.abs(delta),
    merchant: 'Ajuste de saldo',
    note: `Ajuste manual: saldo anterior ${acc.balance}, saldo nuevo ${newBalance}`,
    occurred_at: now,
    source: 'manual',
    payment_method: 'cash',
    status: 'posted',
    dedupe_hash: null,
    created_at: now, 
    updated_at: now, 
    deleted_at: null,
  });
}


==========================================
ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\src\services\security.ts
==========================================
import * as Crypto from 'expo-crypto';
import * as LocalAuth from 'expo-local-authentication';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import { getSetting, setSetting } from './repo';

/**
 * Bloqueo de la app: huella/rostro con PIN de respaldo.
 *  - El PIN nunca se guarda: se guarda un hash salado (SHA-256 repetido) en SecureStore,
 *    que en Android usa el Keystore del hardware.
 *  - Tras 5 intentos fallidos se bloquea el PIN un tiempo creciente.
 *  - Los ajustes (activado, biometrÃ­a, minutos) son de ESTE dispositivo y no se sincronizan.
 */
const PIN_HASH = 'finzen_pin_hash';
const PIN_SALT = 'finzen_pin_salt';
const PIN_LEN = 'finzen_pin_len';
const FAILS = 'finzen_pin_fails';
const LOCK_UNTIL = 'finzen_pin_lock_until';
const ROUNDS = 300;

async function hashPin(pin: string, salt: string): Promise<string> {
  let h = salt + ':' + pin;
  for (let i = 0; i < ROUNDS; i++) h = await Crypto.digestStringAsync(Crypto.CryptoDigestAlgorithm.SHA256, h + salt);
  return h;
}

export const hasPin = async () => (await SecureStore.getItemAsync(PIN_HASH)) !== null;

export async function setPin(pin: string): Promise<void> {
  const salt = Crypto.randomUUID();
  await SecureStore.setItemAsync(PIN_SALT, salt);
  await SecureStore.setItemAsync(PIN_HASH, await hashPin(pin, salt));
  await SecureStore.setItemAsync(PIN_LEN, String(pin.length)); // la pantalla de bloqueo verifica solo al completar el PIN
  await SecureStore.setItemAsync(FAILS, '0');
}

export async function clearPin(): Promise<void> {
  for (const k of [PIN_HASH, PIN_SALT, PIN_LEN, FAILS, LOCK_UNTIL]) await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(k);
}

export async function pinLength(): Promise<number> {
  const n = Number(await SecureStore.getItemAsync(PIN_LEN));
  return n >= 4 && n <= 6 ? n : 6;
}

export type PinResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; lockedForMs: number; attemptsLeft: number };

export async function verifyPin(pin: string): Promise<PinResult> {
  const until = Number((await SecureStore.getItemAsync(LOCK_UNTIL)) ?? 0);
  if (until > Date.now()) return { ok: false, lockedForMs: until - Date.now(), attemptsLeft: 0 };

  const salt = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(PIN_SALT);
  const stored = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(PIN_HASH);
  if (salt && stored && (await hashPin(pin, salt)) === stored) {
    await SecureStore.setItemAsync(FAILS, '0');
    return { ok: true };
  }
  const fails = Number((await SecureStore.getItemAsync(FAILS)) ?? 0) + 1;
  await SecureStore.setItemAsync(FAILS, String(fails));
  if (fails >= 5) {
    const wait = Math.min(30 * 60_000, 30_000 * 2 ** (fails - 5)); // 30 s, 1 min, 2 minâ€¦ tope 30 min
    await SecureStore.setItemAsync(LOCK_UNTIL, String(Date.now() + wait));
    return { ok: false, lockedForMs: wait, attemptsLeft: 0 };
  }
  return { ok: false, lockedForMs: 0, attemptsLeft: 5 - fails };
}

export async function biometricAvailable(): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    return (await LocalAuth.hasHardwareAsync()) && (await LocalAuth.isEnrolledAsync());
  } catch { return false; }
}

export async function authenticateBiometric(): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const r = await LocalAuth.authenticateAsync({
      promptMessage: 'Desbloquear FinZen', cancelLabel: 'Usar PIN', disableDeviceFallback: true,
    });
    return r.success;
  } catch { return false; }
}

// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Preferencias de bloqueo (solo este dispositivo) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export interface LockPrefs { enabled: boolean; biometric: boolean; minutes: number; secureScreen: boolean }

export async function loadLockPrefs(): Promise<LockPrefs> {
  const [e, b, m, s] = await Promise.all(['lock_enabled', 'lock_biometric', 'lock_minutes', 'secure_screen'].map(getSetting));
  return { enabled: e === '1', biometric: b !== '0', minutes: Number(m) || 5, secureScreen: s !== '0' }; // por defecto ACTIVADO: protege la pantalla de capturas y de la vista de apps recientes
}

export async function saveLockPref(key: 'lock_enabled' | 'lock_biometric' | 'lock_minutes' | 'secure_screen', value: string) {
  await setSetting(key, value, 'device');
}

ARCHIVO: \apps\mobile\src\services\sync.ts
==========================================
import 'react-native-url-polyfill/auto';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import { createClient, type SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { SYNCED_TABLES } from '@finzen/db';
import { getDb } from './db';
import { getSetting, setSetting } from './repo';

/**
 * SincronizaciÃ³n OPCIONAL con tu propio proyecto de Supabase (plan gratuito).
 *  - La app funciona 100 % sin esto. Es un respaldo en la nube y para usar varios celulares.
 *  - Cada persona crea su proyecto y pega su URL + anon key en la app (nada va en el repo).
 *  - La contraseÃ±a NO se guarda: solo el refresh token, en SecureStore (Keystore de Android).
 *  - ResoluciÃ³n de conflictos: Ãºltima escritura gana (updated_at); lo aplica un trigger en la nube.
 *  - Solo se envÃ­an las tablas de SYNCED_TABLES; la bitÃ¡cora de notificaciones nunca sale del celular.
 */
const K = { url: 'finzen_sb_url', anon: 'finzen_sb_anon', refresh: 'finzen_sb_refresh', email: 'finzen_sb_email' };

// Padres antes que hijos
const ORDER = ['categories', 'accounts', 'debts', 'debt_installments', 'transactions', 'budgets', 'categorization_rules'] as const;
const SYNCED_SETTINGS = ['palette', 'mode'];

export interface SyncConfig { url: string; anon: string; email: string | null }

export async function getSyncConfig(): Promise<SyncConfig | null> {
  const url = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(K.url);
  const anon = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(K.anon);
  if (!url || !anon) return null;
  return { url, anon, email: await SecureStore.getItemAsync(K.email) };
}

export async function saveSyncConfig(url: string, anon: string): Promise<void> {
  const clean = url.trim().replace(/\/+$/, '');
  if (!/^https:\/\/[a-z0-9-]+\.supabase\.(co|in)$/i.test(clean)) {
    throw new Error('La URL debe verse asÃ­: https://xxxxxxxx.supabase.co');
  }
  await SecureStore.setItemAsync(K.url, clean);
  await SecureStore.setItemAsync(K.anon, anon.trim());
}

export async function clearSyncCredentials(): Promise<void> {
  for (const k of Object.values(K)) await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(k);
}

function makeClient(cfg: SyncConfig): SupabaseClient {
  return createClient(cfg.url, cfg.anon, { auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false, detectSessionInUrl: false } });
}

async function remember(email: string, refresh: string | undefined) {
  await SecureStore.setItemAsync(K.email, email);
  if (refresh) await SecureStore.setItemAsync(K.refresh, refresh);
}

export async function signUp(email: string, password: string): Promise<string> {
  const cfg = await getSyncConfig();
  if (!cfg) throw new Error('Primero guarda la URL y la anon key.');
  const { data, error } = await makeClient(cfg).auth.signUp({ email: email.trim(), password });
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
  if (!data.session) return 'Cuenta creada. Confirma el correo que te llegÃ³ y luego inicia sesiÃ³n.';
  await remember(email.trim(), data.session.refresh_token);
  return 'Cuenta creada y sesiÃ³n iniciada.';
}

export async function signIn(email: string, password: string): Promise<void> {
  const cfg = await getSyncConfig();
  if (!cfg) throw new Error('Primero guarda la URL y la anon key.');
  const { data, error } = await makeClient(cfg).auth.signInWithPassword({ email: email.trim(), password });
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
  await remember(email.trim(), data.session?.refresh_token);
}

export async function signOut(): Promise<void> {
  await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(K.refresh);
}

export async function isSignedIn(): Promise<boolean> {
  return (await getSyncConfig()) !== null && (await SecureStore.getItemAsync(K.refresh)) !== null;
}

async function openSession(): Promise<{ sb: SupabaseClient; userId: string }> {
  const cfg = await getSyncConfig();
  const refresh = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(K.refresh);
  if (!cfg || !refresh) throw new Error('No hay sesiÃ³n de sincronizaciÃ³n. Inicia sesiÃ³n primero.');
  const sb = makeClient(cfg);
  const { data, error } = await sb.auth.refreshSession({ refresh_token: refresh });
  if (error || !data.session) throw new Error('La sesiÃ³n expirÃ³. Inicia sesiÃ³n de nuevo.');
  await SecureStore.setItemAsync(K.refresh, data.session.refresh_token); // los refresh token rotan
  return { sb, userId: data.session.user.id };
}

export async function pendingCount(): Promise<number> {
  const db = await getDb();
  return (await db.getFirstAsync<{ n: number }>('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM sync_queue'))?.n ?? 0;
}

export async function lastSyncAt(): Promise<number | null> {
  const v = await getSetting('sync_last_at');
  return v ? Number(v) : null;
}

/** La primera vez, encola TODO lo que ya existe para subirlo. */
async function enqueueExisting(): Promise<void> {
  if ((await getSetting('sync_initialized')) === '1') return;
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.withTransactionAsync(async () => {
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const t of SYNCED_TABLES) {
      const rows = await db.getAllAsync<{ id: string }>(`SELECT * FROM ${t}`);
      for (const r of rows) {
        await db.runAsync(
          'INSERT INTO sync_queue (table_name,row_id,op,payload,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)',
          [t, r.id, 'upsert', JSON.stringify(r), now],
        );
      }
    }
  });
  await setSetting('sync_initialized', '1', 'device');
}

async function push(sb: SupabaseClient): Promise<number> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const queued = await db.getAllAsync<{ id: number; table_name: string; payload: string | null }>(
    'SELECT id, table_name, payload FROM sync_queue ORDER BY id LIMIT 3000',
  );
  if (queued.length === 0) return 0;

  // Solo importa la versiÃ³n mÃ¡s reciente de cada fila
  const latest = new Map<string, { table: string; row: Record<string, unknown> }>();
  for (const q of queued) {
    if (!q.payload || !(ORDER as readonly string[]).includes(q.table_name)) continue;
    const row = JSON.parse(q.payload) as Record<string, unknown>;
    latest.set(`${q.table_name}/${row.id}`, { table: q.table_name, row });
  }
  for (const table of ORDER) {
    const rows = [...latest.values()].filter((v) => v.table === table).map((v) => v.row);
    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += 200) {
      const { error } = await sb.from(table).upsert(rows.slice(i, i + 200), { onConflict: 'id' });
      if (error) throw new Error(`${table}: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }
  await db.runAsync('DELETE FROM sync_queue WHERE id <= ?', [queued[queued.length - 1]!.id]);
  return latest.size;
}

async function pull(sb: SupabaseClient): Promise<number> {
  const db = await getDb();
  let pulled = 0;
  for (const table of ORDER) {
    const cols = new Set((await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(`PRAGMA table_info(${table})`)).map((c) => c.name));
    let cursor = Number((await getSetting(`sync_cursor_${table}`)) ?? 0);
    for (;;) {
      const { data, error } = await sb.from(table).select('*').gt('updated_at', cursor).order('updated_at', { ascending: true }).limit(500);
      if (error) throw new Error(`${table}: ${error.message}`);
      if (!data || data.length === 0) break;
      await db.withTransactionAsync(async () => {
        await db.execAsync('PRAGMA defer_foreign_keys = ON'); // padre e hijo pueden llegar en cualquier orden
        for (const remote of data as Record<string, any>[]) {
          const local = await db.getFirstAsync<{ updated_at: number }>(`SELECT updated_at FROM ${table} WHERE id=?`, [remote.id]);
          if (local && local.updated_at >= remote.updated_at) continue; // gana la escritura mÃ¡s reciente
          const keys = Object.keys(remote).filter((k) => cols.has(k));
          await db.runAsync(
            `INSERT OR REPLACE INTO ${table} (${keys.join(',')}) VALUES (${keys.map(() => '?').join(',')})`,
            keys.map((k) => remote[k] as string | number | null),
          );
          pulled++;
        }
      });
      cursor = Number((data[data.length - 1] as Record<string, any>).updated_at);
      await setSetting(`sync_cursor_${table}`, String(cursor), 'device');
      if (data.length < 500) break;
    }
  }
  return pulled;
}

/** Paleta y modo se comparten entre tus dispositivos. */
async function syncSettings(sb: SupabaseClient, userId: string): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const { data } = await sb.from('app_settings').select('key,value,updated_at');
  const remote = new Map((data ?? []).map((r: any) => [r.key as string, r as { value: string; updated_at: number }]));
  for (const key of SYNCED_SETTINGS) {
    const local = await db.getFirstAsync<{ value: string; updated_at: number }>('SELECT value,updated_at FROM settings WHERE key=?', [key]);
    const r = remote.get(key);
    if (local && (!r || local.updated_at > r.updated_at)) {
      await sb.from('app_settings').upsert({ user_id: userId, key, value: local.value, updated_at: local.updated_at }, { onConflict: 'user_id,key' });
    } else if (r && (!local || r.updated_at > local.updated_at)) {
      await db.runAsync('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO settings (key,value,scope,updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?)', [key, r.value, 'user', r.updated_at]);
    }
  }
}

let running: Promise<{ pushed: number; pulled: number }> | null = null;

/** Sube los cambios locales y baja los remotos. Seguro de llamar varias veces seguidas. */
export function syncNow(): Promise<{ pushed: number; pulled: number }> {
  running ??= (async () => {
    try {
      const { sb, userId } = await openSession();
      await enqueueExisting();
      const pushed = await push(sb);
      const pulled = await pull(sb);
      await syncSettings(sb, userId);
      await setSetting('sync_last_at', String(Date.now()), 'device');
      await setSetting('sync_last_error', '', 'device');
      return { pushed, pulled };
    } catch (e) {
      await setSetting('sync_last_error', e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e), 'device');
      throw e;
    } finally {
      running = null;
    }
  })();
  return running;
}

let lastAuto = 0;
/** SincronizaciÃ³n silenciosa (como mÃ¡ximo cada 2 minutos) si hay sesiÃ³n. Nunca lanza error. */
export async function autoSync(): Promise<void> {
  if (Date.now() - lastAuto < 120_000) return;
  lastAuto = Date.now();
  try { if (await isSignedIn()) await syncNow(); } catch { /* sin internet o sesiÃ³n caducada: se reintenta luego */ }
}

/** Borra tus datos de la nube (no borra tu usuario: eso se hace en el panel de Supabase). */
export async function deleteRemoteData(): Promise<void> {
  const { sb } = await openSession();
  const { error } = await sb.rpc('delete_my_data');
  if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
}
